*Matthew Perlman,James Beetham,Niels Da Vitoria Lobo,Amrit Singh Bedi,Mubarak Shah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了利用推理时方法（约束迭代精炼框架）在现成的视觉语言模型（VLM）中解锁SVG生成能力，发现约束解码能提高编译成功率，但迭代精炼暴露了模型在视觉推理和自我纠正方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SVG在现代视觉生态中至关重要，但当前先进的生成模型几乎完全聚焦于栅格化图像，缺乏对SVG生成的研究。作者希望探索能否通过推理时方法，在不重新训练的情况下让现成的VLM具备SVG生成能力。

Method: 作者提出并系统评估了一个约束迭代精炼框架，该框架结合了视觉反馈、结构化编辑和约束解码三个关键技术，并在多个VLM和多种生成设置下进行实验来表征现成VLM在SVG生成任务上的能力与局限。

Result: 研究发现：约束解码能够显著提升SVG编译成功率；迭代精炼过程则暴露出当前VLM在视觉推理和自纠错能力方面的明显缺陷。

Conclusion: 推理时方法在将通用VLM适配到SVG生成任务上既展现出潜力，也揭示了当前模型的固有局限性，为未来改进视觉生成中的自纠错能力提供了方向。

Abstract: Scalable Vector Graphics (SVGs) power much of the modern visual ecosystem, yet state-of-the-art generative models focus almost entirely on rasterized images. We explore whether inference-time methods can unlock SVG generation capabilities in off-the-shelf vision-language models (VLMs). We systematically evaluate a constrained iterative refinement harness that combines visual feedback, structured editing, and constrained decoding to characterize the capabilities and limitations of current VLMs for SVG generation. Across multiple VLMs and generation settings, we find that constrained decoding improves compilation success rates, while iterative refinement reveals a deficit in visual reasoning and self-correction. Our results highlight both the promise and current limitations of using inference-time methods to adapt general-purpose VLMs for SVG generation.

</details>


### [13] [Multi-Sensor Mapping of Vulnerable Urban Settlements Using SAR, Multispectral, and Hyperspectral Imagery: A Case Study in Córdoba, Argentina](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28680)
*Luigi Russo,Anabella Ferral,Silvia Liberata Ullo,Paolo Gamba*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多传感器深度学习框架，融合PlanetScope多光谱影像、COSMO-SkyMed SAR数据和PRISMA高光谱数据，用于阿根廷科尔多瓦市的非正规住区（贫民区）似然度制图。通过四种地理分区折叠进行补丁级分类评估，系统比较了不同融合策略（早期、中期、晚期融合）。结果表明晚期融合+高光谱支持在分类性能与空间选择性上取得最佳平衡，且PRISMA提供了互补的光谱信息。外部市政脆弱性图层和热分析进一步验证了检测结果与更广泛的城市脆弱性模式的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非正规住区在快速扩张的城市中构成重大挑战，但由于其异质性外观和不完整的官方清单，从地球观测数据中识别它们仍然困难。本文旨在通过多传感器数据融合来解决这一难题，提高非正规住区识别的准确性和空间选择性。

Method: 采用多传感器深度学习框架：融合高分辨率PlanetScope多光谱影像、COSMO-SkyMed合成孔径雷达数据和中等分辨率PRISMA高光谱观测。问题被构架为补丁级分类任务，以官方ReNaBaP清单为参考，通过四个地理分区折叠评估。系统比较了SAR-only和MS-only基线、加入PRISMA支持的配置，以及早期融合、中期融合和晚期融合三种融合策略。

Result: 晚期融合+高光谱支持提供了分类性能与空间选择性之间最佳的整体平衡。PRISMA贡献了与高分辨率MS和SAR表示互补的光谱信息。外部市政脆弱性图层显示许多表观误报与更广泛的城市脆弱区域重叠。热分析表明ReNaBaP住区在热浪事件期间表面温度显著高于周围环境，呈现局部表面热放大效应。

Conclusion: 多传感器地球观测融合既能支持ReNaBaP住区的制图，也能帮助解释更广泛的城市脆弱性模式，为非正规住区识别和城市脆弱性评估提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Informal settlements represent a major urban challenge in rapidly expanding cities, yet their identification from Earth Observation (EO) data remains difficult because of their heterogeneous appearance and incomplete official inventories. This work presents a multi-sensor deep learning (DL) framework for slum-likelihood mapping in Córdoba, Argentina, integrating high-resolution PlanetScope multispectral (MS) imagery, COSMO-SkyMed (CSK) Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) data, and medium-resolution PRISMA hyperspectral (HS) observations. The problem is formulated as a patch-level classification task using the official Registro Nacional de Barrios Populares (ReNaBaP) inventory as reference, and the models are evaluated through four geographically partitioned folds. SAR-only and MS-only baselines, their configurations with PRISMA HS support, and early fusion (EF), middle fusion (MF), and late fusion (LF) strategies are systematically compared. Results show that LF+HS provides the best overall balance between classification performance and spatial selectivity, while PRISMA contributes complementary spectral information alongside the higher-resolution MS and SAR representations. Beyond the standard evaluation against ReNaBaP, an external municipal vulnerability layer is used to interpret detections outside the official polygons, showing that several apparent false positives overlap broader vulnerable urban areas. Thermal analysis further shows that ReNaBaP settlements exhibit significantly higher surface temperatures than their immediate surroundings during a heatwave event, indicating localised surface-heat amplification. Taken together, these results suggest that multi-sensor EO fusion can support both the mapping of ReNaBaP settlements and the interpretation of broader urban vulnerability patterns.

</details>


### [14] [CARD: Calibration via Agreement in Reverse Diffusion for Out-of-Domain MRI Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28681)
*Jiaheng Dai,Weidong Guo,Qingbiao Li,Jie Xu,Yi Guo,Yuanyuan Wang,Zeju Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出CARD（逆向扩散一致性校准）方法，利用分类扩散模型的生成形态先验与反向去噪轨迹中的类别分布一致性，为医学图像分割在域偏移下提供超越终端预测的可信度证据，实现逐像素温度校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率校准在正常场景下能对齐模型置信度与预测精度，但在域偏移下，伪影和未见协议会产生高置信度的错误。现有事后校准方法依赖预测熵、logit模式或增强响应等终端预测的代理量，而终端预测正是被域偏移破坏的量，因此需要从终端预测之外寻找可靠性证据。

Method: 利用分类扩散模型的两大特性：(1)生成形态先验在表观被破坏时保持容量受限的参考不变，其与主分割器的分歧可突出主模型错误；(2)每个反向扩散步骤都产生类别分布，可区分持续性分歧与瞬时性偏差。将轨迹上聚合的分歧度映射为逐像素、跨类别的温度场，在保持分割结果不变的前提下调整置信度。

Result: 该分歧度与Dice的相关性达0.788，远高于对照判别模型的0.521。在心脏、前列腺和脑部MRI域偏移场景中，CARD在49项比较中优于最强基线45项，大幅降低了校准误差。

Conclusion: CARD验证了超越终端预测的可靠性证据（逆向扩散中的分歧度）在域偏移下校准分割置信度的有效性，既提升了校准效果又不改变分割结果本身，为医学图像分割的不确定性建模提供了新思路。

Abstract: Probability calibration aligns model confidence with predictive accuracy, enabling clinicians to identify unreliable segmentation regions. This alignment breaks down under domain shift, where artifacts and unseen protocols produce confident errors. Existing post-hoc methods adapt the correction at test time, conditioning on predictive entropy, the logit pattern, or augmentation response, but each proxy is read from the terminal prediction, the very quantity that shift corrupts. This motivates reliability evidence beyond the terminal prediction, which categorical diffusion provides in two ways. First, a generative shape prior keeps a capacity-limited reference intact when appearance is corrupted, so its disagreement with the primary segmentor highlights primary-model errors. Second, every reverse step yields a class distribution, separating persistent disagreement from transient discrepancy. Aggregated over the trajectory, this disagreement correlates with Dice at 0.788, against 0.521 for a matched discriminative control. We therefore propose CARD (Calibration via Agreement in Reverse Diffusion), which maps the temporal aggregate of this disagreement to a temperature field applied per pixel across all classes, so that confidence changes while the segmentation does not. Across cardiac, prostate and brain MRI shifts, CARD lowers calibration error in 45 of 49 comparisons against the strongest baseline in each setting.

</details>


### [15] [Distributed Semantic Segmentation With Improved Rate-Distortion Trade-Off](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28684)
*Danish Nazir,Timo Bartels,Thorsten Bagdonat,Tim Fingscheidt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对分布式深度神经网络中的语义分割任务，提出了两种新型源编解码器，在极低比特率下（低于0.2或0.03 bpp）实现了优于现有方法的率失真性能，并在ADE20K和Cityscapes数据集上取得了最先进的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式DNN方案将编码器部署在边缘设备、解码器部署在云端，但传统源编解码器受限特定类型且网络架构探索不足，导致低比特率下率失真性能次优。

Method: 提出两种新型源编解码器，专为分布式语义分割设计，在边缘与云之间实现极低比特率的高效特征传输，同时改善率失真性能。

Result: 在ADE20K数据集上低于0.2 bpp、Cityscapes数据集上低于0.03 bpp时，使用mIoU指标取得了最先进的分布式语义分割性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的源编解码器在极低比特率条件下同时提升了传输效率和语义分割质量，展示了在资源受限的边缘-云分布式系统中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Distributed deep neural networks (DNNs) for dense perception tasks such as semantic segmentation execute an encoder DNN on edge devices, and a decoder DNN typically on a large-scale cloud platform with a particular constraint on transmission bitrate. Recent works employ source codecs to enable bitrate-efficient transmission between the edge device and the cloud. However, as these approaches are typically bound to a particular type of source codec and alternative network architectures are often not explored, this results in a suboptimal rate-distortion (RD) trade-off in the low-bitrate regime. In this work, we propose two novel source codecs that \textit{enable extremely low bitrates, while improving RD performance}. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed source codecs by achieving state-of-the-art performance in distributed semantic segmentation at below 0.2 (0.03) bits per pixel, measured using the mean intersection-over-union metric on ADE20K (Cityscapes).

</details>


### [16] [Data Diversity, Not Frequency Invariance: A Controlled and Self-Audited Study of Compression-Robust Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28685)
*Abbas Aliyev,Samir Rustamov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过严格的预注册对照实验测试了"频率特征与压缩不变表征学习"对深度换脸检测（deepfake detection）的假设，结果呈阴性：在FaceForensics++测试集上，普通EfficientNet-B0在所有压缩级别上均优于作者提出的CAFRL架构（CRF 40时AUC高出3.66个百分点）。自我审计发现初始实验存在四个方法偏差，修复后表明差距源于配方（recipe）而非架构。频率路径在该融合框架下没有边际价值，对抗压缩不变分支没有提升。真正的压缩鲁棒性来自数据多样性——真实码率变体比合成JPEG增强高出7.3个AUC点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度换脸检测在视频压缩后性能显著下降，学术界普遍认为频率特征和压缩不变表征学习（如CAFRL：块DCT、FFT相位流、压缩级别条件频带注意力、梯度反转对抗压缩不变性）是解决该问题的关键。但这一假设缺乏严格的对照验证，作者旨在通过预注册协议和匹配对照实验来检验该假设是否成立。

Method: 作者提出CAFRL架构，包含block-DCT和FFT相位双流、压缩级别条件频带注意力、以及梯度反转的对抗压缩不变性分支。实验采用预注册方案，使用容量和增强策略均匹配的对照（普通EfficientNet-B0多质量数据训练），在FaceForensics++测试集各压缩级别上比较。并对阴性结果进行自我审计，识别四个偏置缺陷后进行预指定的修复重测。对比真实码率变体与合成JPEG增强的数据多样性效果。

Result: CAFRL在所有压缩级别上逊于普通EfficientNet-B0（CRF 40时AUC低3.66分）。修复四个配方缺陷后，基线配方比原始附带配方提升了3.96个AUC点，说明差异源于配方而非架构缺陷。频率路径单独有判别力（AUC 0.91-0.98）但在融合中无边际价值，两个特征宽度下压缩对比区间均含零。对抗分支无增益且损害了自身条件估计器。真实码率变体比合成JPEG增强高7.3个AUC点。

Conclusion: 频率压缩不变性假设在受控条件下未获得支持——性能差距源于训练配方而非频率架构。压缩鲁棒性主要来自数据多样性（真实码率变体），而非架构层面的频率分析或对抗不变性。建议研究者在设计检测器时，首先匹配训练配方和模型容量，并以编码器多样性而非合成增强来获取压缩鲁棒性。证据局限于FaceForensics++系列、GAN时代和单编码器。

Abstract: Frequency features and compression-invariant representation learning are widely assumed to be key to deepfake detection that survives video compression. We test this with CAFRL - block-DCT and FFT-phase streams, compression-level-conditioned band attention, and adversarial (gradient-reversal) compression invariance - and report a controlled negative. Under a pre-registered protocol with capacity- and augmentation-matched controls, a plain EfficientNet-B0 on multi-quality data beat CAFRL as specified at every compression level on the FaceForensics++ test split, by 3.66 AUC points at CRF 40 (paired, single seed). A self-audit of our own negative found four defects biased against the frequency hypothesis, and pre-specified re-tests repairing all four showed the deficit to be a recipe artifact, not an architecture failure: the baseline recipe recovered 3.96 points over the matching shipped-recipe variant. The frequency path made no detectable difference: discriminative alone (standalone validation AUC 0.91-0.98 late in training) but of no marginal value under this fusion, at two feature widths of one 4.0 M trunk, every seed-pooled interval for the intra-dataset compression contrasts including zero; on the single held-out manipulation tested, the fair variants sat below the plain backbone. The adversarial branch, as specified, added nothing and degraded its own conditioning estimator; at the fair recipe it is untested. Robustness under single-pass H.264 re-encoding came instead from data diversity: real constant-rate-factor variants beat synthetic JPEG augmentation by 7.3 points (single runs, non-overlapping intervals). The evidence is FaceForensics++-family, GAN-era and single-codec. Match controls on training recipe as well as capacity, and buy compression robustness with codec diversity before architecture.

</details>


### [17] [FLM: Frequency-Aware Language Models for Generative Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28687)
*Jiarun Chen,Kejun Wu,Li Li,Chengtao Cai,Zhengguo Li,Chia-Wen Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FLM，一种频率感知语言模型，通过频域概率建模和自回归预测实现图像有损压缩，同时保留确定性重建，在保持高保真度的同时提升压缩效率，并在多个基准上超越传统和生成式压缩方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式压缩模型虽能在低码率下提升性能，但生成纹理和语义细节可能偏离源内容，损害重建保真度。作者希望在不牺牲重建质量的前提下提升压缩效率。

Method: FLM将输入图像转换为量化DCT系数，通过宏块系数标记化组织为离散序列；利用语言模型进行下一系数预测，自回归估计逐词条件概率分布用于算术编码；编码端生成紧凑比特流，解码端联合LLM和算术解码器恢复频域数据并逆变换重建图像。结合任务特化频域数据集和两阶段微调策略支持多码率设置。

Result: FLM在率失真性能上超越传统和生成式有损压缩方法。相比JPEG基线，在Kodak、Tecnick和CLIC2020上分别获得3.30 dB、3.83 dB和3.80 dB的BD-PSNR增益，同时有效提升语义保真度并抑制块效应，并在无损重压缩任务中也表现竞争性。

Conclusion: FLM展示了频率感知语言建模与算术编码结合在图像压缩中的潜力，既能利用学习先验提升压缩效率，又通过确定性重建避免生成式方法的内容偏离问题，是一种通用且兼容多种压缩框架的解决方案。

Abstract: Generative models have significantly improved the performance ceiling of image lossy compression at low bitrates by exploiting learned priors. However, the generated textures and semantic details may deviate from the source content, thereby affecting the fidelity of image reconstruction. To solve these challenges, we propose FLM, a frequency-aware language model that improves compression efficiency through frequency-domain probabilistic modeling while retaining deterministic reconstruction. At the encoder, the input image is transformed into quantized DCT coefficients, which are organized into discrete sequences using macroblock-based coefficient tokenization. FLM then performs next-coefficient prediction to autoregressively estimate token-wise conditional probability distributions for arithmetic coding, thereby generating a compact bitstream. At the decoder, the LLM and arithmetic decoder jointly recover the frequency-domain data, followed by inverse transformations for image reconstruction. A task-specific frequency-domain dataset and a two-stage fine-tuning strategy are further developed to enable the model to operate across multiple bitrate settings. FLM is a versatile compressor that is compatible with both lossy compression and lossless JPEG recompression frameworks. Experiments show that FLM exceeds conventional and generative lossy compression methods in rate-distortion performance. FLM achieves BD-PSNR gains of 3.30 dB, 3.83 dB, and 3.80 dB than JPEG baseline on Kodak, Tecnick, and CLIC2020, respectively. Better qualitative quality of FLM can be achieved in improving semantically high fidelity and suppressing blocking artifacts. FLM is also validated to be applicable to the lossless recompression task with competitive performance.

</details>


### [18] [Projection-Aware End-to-End Learned Video Compression for 360-Degree Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28689)
*Niloofar Maani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文研究了投影格式对360度视频端到端神经压缩性能的影响。使用scale-space flow模型和JVET 360Lib工具，评估了七种投影格式，发现等距柱状投影（ERP）和填充等距柱状投影（PEP）在神经压缩中效率最高，而基于立方体映射的投影格式表现较差，这与传统编解码器（HM-16.16）的结果相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 360度视频需要将球面内容映射为二维投影才能进行压缩编码，投影格式选择会影响空间连续性、采样均匀性、运动估计和压缩效率。以往研究主要针对传统编解码器，缺乏对学习型神经压缩方法中投影格式影响的系统探究。

Method: 使用scale-space flow神经压缩模型，选取JVET 360Lib支持的七种投影格式，按照JVET通用测试条件，将每个序列从源等距柱状投影转换到编码投影，在多码率点压缩、重建并转换回原格式。评估指标包括PSNR、球面PSNR、加权球面PSNR和Bjøntegaard delta rate，同时比较了可微的投影转换-神经压缩-逆投影流水线与360Lib传统方案。

Result: 等距柱状投影（ERP）和填充等距柱状投影（PEP）在scale-space flow模型下压缩效率最高，而基于立方体映射和菱形十二面体的投影效率较低。这与传统HM-16.16编解码器的结论相反——后者中基于立方体的投影（特别是EAC和ACP）优于等距柱状投影。

Conclusion: 投影格式的压缩效率依赖于具体编解码器。基于光流的神经模型受益于单面投影的空间连续性，而基于块的混合编解码器更能适应多面布局。该发现为学习型360度视频压缩的投影格式选择提供了指导。

Abstract: 360-degree video supports immersive applications such as virtual reality, autonomous driving, and education. Because spherical content cannot be processed directly by conventional video codecs, it must first be mapped to a two-dimensional projection. Projection choice affects spatial continuity, sampling uniformity, motion estimation, and compression efficiency.
  This thesis investigates how projection format influences end-to-end neural compression of 360-degree video. Seven formats supported by JVET 360Lib are evaluated using the scale-space flow model, JVET test sequences, and common test conditions. Each sequence is converted from its source equirectangular projection to a coding projection, compressed at multiple rate points, reconstructed, and converted back. Performance is assessed using PSNR, spherical PSNR, weighted spherical PSNR, and Bjøntegaard delta rate. A differentiable pipeline combining projection conversion, neural compression, and inverse projection is also compared with 360Lib.
  Results show that equirectangular and padded equirectangular projections provide the highest compression efficiency with the scale-space flow model, while cubemap-based and rhombic dodecahedron projections are less effective. This differs from the conventional HM-16.16 codec, for which cubemap-based formats, particularly equi-angular and adjusted cubemap projections, outperform equirectangular formats. Neural models based on optical flow benefit from the spatial continuity of single-face projections, whereas block-based hybrid codecs better accommodate multi-face layouts. These findings show that projection efficiency is codec-dependent and provide guidance for selecting projections for learning-based 360-degree video compression.

</details>


### [19] [Defending Wearable VLMs Against Private Attribute Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28691)
*Zhimin Li,Pan Wang,Jingxian Chen,Yuantao Tang,Anthony Chen,Qian Lou,Jingtong Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了可穿戴VLM管道中视觉token传输的隐私泄露问题，提出Token-Guided Attribute Privacy (TGAP)方法，在不牺牲实用性的前提下大幅降低隐私属性识别的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴VLM系统通过接收用户的任务驱动问题并提供视觉辅助，但同一份第一人称视角的视觉证据可能泄露佩戴者或旁人的隐私属性。尽管已有研究关注最终文本响应中的隐私，但中间视觉token的泄露面尚未得到充分研究——即使最终文本响应无害，攻击者或不可信的下游组件仍可从视觉token中恢复隐私属性。

Method: 构建了一个包含3,221条图像-问题记录的配对隐私-效用基准数据集，标注了位置、收入、性别、兴趣四类隐私标签。提出TGAP方法，这是一种pre-LLM token解纠缠器，在视觉token离开可信边界前学习残差变换。TGAP结合了效用保持、身份正则化、语义隐私抑制和图像驱动表示抑制，避免了粗粒度硬掩码或注意力掩码带来的效用损失。

Result: 在源模型评估基准上，TGAP将隐私识别准确率从56.7%降至7.4%（绝对下降49.3%），同时保持放松效用为74.4%。

Conclusion: 保护紧凑的token接口是迈向隐私保护的穿戴式多模态AI的实用路径，表明在可信设备边界内对视觉token进行隐私处理后传输，可以在实用性与隐私保护之间取得有效平衡。

Abstract: Wearable VLM pipelines promise continuous multimodal assistance from egocentric visual capture: a user asks a task-driven question about the surrounding scene, and the system uses compact visual tokens to support language reasoning. The challenge motivating this work is that the same egocentric evidence needed for useful assistance can also reveal private attributes about the wearer or nearby bystanders. We investigate this as a joint privacy-utility problem for split VLM inference, where visual encoding occurs within a trusted device boundary but intermediate visual tokens may be transmitted to downstream reasoning components. This exposes an understudied leakage surface: even when final textual responses are benign, external attackers or untrusted downstream components can recover private attributes from transmitted visual tokens. To evaluate this tension, we construct a paired privacy-utility benchmark with 3,221 image-question records, each paired with a utility question and privacy labels covering location, income, sex, and interests. We further propose Token-Guided Attribute Privacy (TGAP), a pre-LLM token disentangler that learns a residual transformation of visual tokens before they leave the trusted boundary. TGAP combines utility preservation, identity regularization, semantic privacy suppression, and image-driven representation suppression, avoiding the utility loss caused by coarse hard or attention masking. On the benchmark used for source-model evaluation, TGAP reduces privacy accuracy from 56.7\% to 7.4\%, a 49.3\% absolute drop, while maintaining relaxed utility at 74.4\%. These results suggest that securing the compact token interface is a practical path toward privacy-preserving wearable multimodal AI.

</details>


### [20] [Growing a Stand, Not a Tree: Joint Canopy Generation Reproduces Crown Shyness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28692)
*Guang Yang,Fengchen Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了"冠层羞避"（crown shyness）这一森林冠层中的集群现象，将其作为生成建模的探针，提出用流匹配模型进行集合级冠层生成，通过注意力机制在树间建立耦合，证明该联合生成方法比逐树独立生成方法误差减半，并在热带橡树林实测数据上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冠层羞避现象中，相邻树冠之间保持间隙，这种结构属于整个林分而非任何单棵树。这提出了一个生成建模的核心问题：学习模型能否生成一组目标，其定义性结构仅存在于目标之间？传统逐树生成方法无法捕捉这种涌现性的集合级结构。

Method: 将林分级冠层生成建模为集合生成问题，采用流匹配（flow-matching）模型，其中树间注意力是耦合产生的唯一通道。模型在资源竞争模拟生成的林分上进行训练，该模拟被证明不可化简为单棵树的几何特征。同时使用单一标定标量参数，对热带橡树林的野外测量数据进行标定。

Result: 联合模型将间隙分布误差较同容量逐树生成模型减半，且在训练范围外的茎密度下优势仍然保持。在热带橡树林野外数据上，单一标定标量即可实现间隙幅度和冠层不对称性的留出数据一致性。间隙的方向统计由茎的位置而非生长规则控制，标定茎抖动后与野外数据吻合。

Conclusion: 冠层羞避在仿真和学习的模型中都是林分的整体属性而非单棵树的属性。集合级生成建模能够有效捕捉仅存在于目标之间的耦合结构，为生成建模中处理此类涌现性交互模式提供了有力范式。

Abstract: In closed forests, neighboring tree crowns often stop short of touching, leaving a network of narrow gaps known as crown shyness. The pattern belongs to the stand rather than to any single tree, which makes it a natural probe of a question in generative modeling: can a learned model produce a set of objects whose defining structure exists only between them? We formulate stand-level canopy generation as set generation with a flow-matching model, in which attention between trees is the only channel through which coupling can arise. Trained on stands grown by a resource-competition simulation that is provably not reducible to per-tree geometry, the joint model halves the clearance distribution error of an identical-capacity model that generates each tree alone, and the advantage persists at stem densities outside the training range. Against field measurements of a tropical oak forest, a single calibrated scalar yields held-out agreement in gap magnitude and crown asymmetry. The directional statistics of the gaps are controlled by stem placement rather than by the growth rule, and match the field once stem jitter is calibrated. Crown shyness, in both the simulation and the learned model, is a property of the stand and not of the tree.

</details>


### [21] [SNF-Bench: Separating Static Drift from Natural Flow in Long-Horizon Fixed-Camera Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28694)
*Matiur Rahman Minar,Seunghun Oh,Ganghyeon Jeong,Unsang Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SNF-Bench，一个针对固定相机长时程视频生成的评估框架，将场景划分为静态支撑与动态流动，分别报告静态保真度、流动持久性（含绝对幅度）与漂移泄漏三个指标，而非单一分数。通过机械性验证注入已知漂移和冻结，实验发现整体帧运动与静态区域漂移对同一输出产生几乎相反的排序，揭示了现有评估指标的歧义问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时程视频生成评估使用整体帧指标，同时奖励运动和时间一致性。但对固定相机的自然场景，水体、火焰、烟雾、雨的运动是期望的，而背景运动是错误，导致系统可能在场景漂移时仍得分高，或流动停滞时因一致性得分高，现有指标无法区分这两种情况。

Method: SNF-Bench将场景划分为静态支撑和动态流动两部分，分别报告静态保真度、流动持久性（含绝对幅度）和漂移泄漏三个独立指标。每个指标通过机械性验证：向真实生成中注入已知严重程度的全局平移、旋转、缩放漂移和渐进式后期冻结，要求各指标按声明方向响应，并对不针对的损坏保持选择性。审计公开长时程文本条件checkpoint，并包含图像条件赛道和部署敏感性面板。

Result: 在最大受控平移下，fBD和NBF分别升至基线的1.86倍和1.32倍，而整体帧Dynamic Degree仅达1.07倍——即整体指标奖励了损坏。实验发现整体帧运动与静态区域漂移对相同输出产生几乎相反的排序，说明SNF-Bench能准确区分运动发生位置及其持久性。

Conclusion: SNF-Bench测量的是运动发生的位置以及运动是否持久，而非物理真实性。它为固定相机长时程视频生成提供了更精细、可机械性验证的评估框架，能够揭示并解决现有整体帧指标在运动与静态漂移间的歧义问题。

Abstract: Long-horizon video generation is evaluated with whole-frame metrics that reward motion and temporal consistency. For fixed-camera nature scenes this creates an ambiguity: motion of water, fire, smoke, or rain is desirable, whereas motion of the background is an error. A system can therefore score well on motion while its scene drifts, or on consistency while its flow stagnates. We introduce SNF-Bench, an evaluation framework for long-horizon fixed-camera generation that partitions each scene into static support and dynamic flow and reports static fidelity, flow persistence with absolute magnitude, and drift leakage separately, never as one score. Drift leakage is interpretive context rather than a headline measurement. Each factor is validated mechanistically rather than by correlation with preference: we inject global translation, rotation, and scale drift and progressive late freezing at known severity into real generations, and require each factor to respond in its stated direction and to remain selective against corruptions it does not target. Auditing publicly released long-horizon text-conditioned checkpoints under one recorded common inference configuration, plus an image-conditioned track with released-pipeline references and a deployment-sensitivity panel, we find that whole-frame motion and static-region drift induce near-opposite orderings of the same outputs. At maximum controlled translation, fBD and NBF rise to $1.86\times$ and $1.32\times$ baseline, but whole-frame Dynamic Degree reaches only $1.07\times$---rewarding the corruption. SNF-Bench measures where motion occurs and whether it persists; it does not measure physical realism. Project page: https://minar09.github.io/snfbench/.

</details>


### [22] [Weaving Visual Narratives: Agentic Image Bundle Composition Beyond Atomic Visual Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28695)
*Rong Shan,Tianyi Xu,Congmin Zheng,Wenteng Chen,Jiachen Zhu,Junjie Wu,Teng Wang,Weiwen Liu,Changwang Zhang,Weinan Zhang,Jun Wang,Jianghao Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出图像束组合（IBC）新范式，从单张图像排序转向动态组合图像束；构建了首个IBCBench基准数据集（109,467张图像和667个验证查询），并提出了BundleWeaver智能体框架，通过LLM自适应性搜索缺失关系角色和VLM进行整体束验证，在组合空间中有效导航并取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像检索被形式化为逐点匹配问题，每张候选图像孤立评分。然而这种原子化范式无法捕捉个人相册中人类搜索意图的复杂性——用户通常寻求由结构关系绑定的紧凑视觉故事，而非孤立的快照。目标束并非预先定义，这带来了严重的组合爆炸挑战，需要建模不可分解的联合相关性。

Method: （1）构建IBCBench基准数据集，包含109,467张图像和667个验证查询，通过半自动化验证流程构建；（2）提出BundleWeaver智能体框架，将IBC重构为查询条件化的增量超边发现问题，利用大语言模型自适应搜索缺失的关系角色，并利用视觉语言模型进行整体束级验证，以有效导航组合空间。

Result: 实验表明，最先进的嵌入模型和静态分解-再排序范式存在关系盲区（relational blindness），而BundleWeaver取得了显著的性能提升，验证了从原子化评分向动态关系组合转变的必要性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，图像检索应从原子化的逐点匹配转向动态的关系组合范式，BundleWeaver为这一新范式提供了有效解决方案，同时也为社区提供了基准数据集和代码以推动后续研究。

Abstract: Image retrieval has traditionally been formulated as a point-wise matching problem, where each candidate image is scored in isolation. However, this atomic paradigm fails to capture the complexity of human search intent within personal photo collections, where users often seek compact visual stories bound by structural relations rather than isolated snapshots. To address this limitation, we introduce **Image Bundle Composition (IBC)**, a novel paradigm that shifts the objective from ranking individual images to dynamically composing cohesive image bundles from a massive, unstructured photo pool. Since target bundles are not predefined, IBC presents a severe combinatorial explosion challenge and demands modeling non-decomposable joint relevance. To establish this paradigm, we construct **IBCBench**, the first IBC benchmark dataset containing 109,467 images and 667 verified queries, built via a semi-automated verification pipeline. Furthermore, we propose **BundleWeaver**, an agentic framework that reformulates IBC as query-conditioned incremental hyperedge discovery. By employing a Large Language Model to adaptively search for missing relational roles and utilizing a Vision-Language Model for whole-bundle verification, BundleWeaver effectively navigates the combinatorial space. Extensive experiments demonstrate that while state-of-the-art embedding models and static decompose-and-rerank paradigms suffer from relational blindness, BundleWeaver achieves substantial performance gains, highlighting the necessity of shifting from atomic scoring to dynamic relational composition. Our dataset and code are available.

</details>


### [23] [Instruction Distillation: Text Instructions as Visual Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28696)
*Hardik Jindal,Soumyabrata Pal,Sayak Ray Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出指令蒸馏（Instruction Distillation）方法，通过让多模态大语言模型（MLLM）为每张训练图像生成结构化识别指令，将视觉上下文学习中昂贵的图像示例替换为低成本的文本指令，在保持甚至提升分类性能的同时，将推理标记数减少2.9倍、推理延迟降低3.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉上下文学习（ICL）在细粒度视觉分类中虽然有效，但每个检索到的图像示例需要消耗数百个上下文标记，在大K设置下推理成本过高。现有方法仅为每个类别生成单一描述，丢失了类内视觉多样性。

Method: 提出指令蒸馏（Instruction Distillation）：离线过程中由MLLM为每张训练图像生成结构化识别指令，编码一般外观线索、与视觉相似类别的区分特征以及常见混淆点。推理时研究了五种配置：零样本、图像ICL、仅指令ICL，以及两种将检索邻居在图像和指令间分配的混合变体，共同使用单个CLIP检索索引。

Result: 在七个细粒度基准和两个MLLM骨干上，基于指令的pipeline在K=1时匹配或超越图像ICL，在K=5时每查询标记减少2.9倍、推理延迟降低3.3倍。混合配置表明视觉和文本ICL信号互补，同时提供可提升上下文质量。

Conclusion: 指令蒸馏为大规模视觉上下文学习提供了一种高效且有效的替代方案，验证了按训练图像生成指令而非按类别生成描述的必要性，并揭示了视觉与文本ICL信号的互补性。

Abstract: Visual in-context learning (ICL) with multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is effective for fine-grained visual classification, but each retrieved image example consumes several hundred context tokens, making large-$K$ settings prohibitively expensive at inference scale. We propose Instruction Distillation: an offline procedure in which the MLLM itself generates, for each individual training image, a structured identification instruction encoding general appearance cues, features that differentiate the class from visually similar ones, and a common confusion point. Unlike prior work that produces a single description per class, our instructions are generated per training image, preserving the intra-class visual diversity that per-class descriptions collapse. At inference time, we study five configurations sharing a single CLIP retrieval index: zero-shot, image ICL, instruction-only ICL, and two hybrid variants in which retrieved neighbors are split between images and instructions. Across seven fine-grained benchmarks and two MLLM backbones, instruction based pipelines match, or exceeds image ICL at $K{=}1$ and reduces per-query tokens by $2.9\times$ and inference latency by $3.3\times$ at $K{=}5$. Hybrid configurations further show that visual and textual ICL signals are complementary, images give visual patterns to learn and see, while instructions give explicit rules and logic. When both of these are provided, the quality of context improves, which is noticeable in the performance.

</details>


### [24] [State-Conditioned Visual Evidence Retrieval for Fine-Grained Perception in Document Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28698)
*Mingxu Chai,Chenyu Liu,Ziyu Shen,Jiazheng Zhang,Kaidi Zhang,Ruoyu Chen,Jun Long,Jihua Kang,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SCVER方法，将文档解析中的视觉感知建模为状态条件化的视觉证据检索，通过粗到细的设计实现按需访问高分辨率细节，并引入SGLO稳定检索学习过程，在文档解析基准上取得了更好的精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM文档解析方法依赖全局压缩的视觉token，细粒度细节被纠缠在单一表示中，且每个解码步骤都需要完整访问这些表示，计算效率低下。然而视觉证据通常是局部且依赖于当前解码状态的，这种不匹配导致效率浪费。

Method: 将感知建模为状态条件化视觉证据检索（SCVER），模型在紧凑全局表示上进行粗粒度结构理解，并根据当前token状态检索少量相关的高分辨率区域。同时引入空间引导学习目标（SGLO）来稳定检索过程的学习。

Result: 在文档解析基准上，SCVER在降低输入分辨率的情况下提升了鲁棒性，并实现了更好的精度-效率权衡，验证了按需视觉证据检索对细粒度感知的有效性。

Conclusion: 按需检索的粗到细设计能够有效缓解全局压缩表示承载过多细粒度信息的负担，为VLM在细粒度感知任务上提供了更高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Compared with typical vision-language tasks, document parsing places stronger demands on fine-grained visual perception. Existing vision-language model (VLM)-based parsing approaches rely on globally compressed visual tokens, where fine-grained details are entangled within a single representation and repeatedly accessed during decoding. However, we observe that the visual evidence for each prediction is typically localized and conditioned on the current decoding state, whereas such representations must be accessed in full at every decoding step, resulting in inefficient computation. To address this mismatch, we formulate perception as state-conditioned visual evidence retrieval (SCVER) during autoregressive decoding. The model operates on a compact global representation for coarse structure and retrieves a small set of relevant high-resolution regions conditioned on the current token state. This coarse-to-fine design enables on-demand access to fine-grained visual cues, relieving globally shared representations from encoding all fine-grained details. We further find that learning such state-conditioned retrieval in VLMs is challenging and unstable. To stabilize this process, we introduce a Spatially-Guided Learning Objective (SGLO) to guide the retrieval process. Experiments on document parsing benchmarks show that SCVER improves robustness under reduced input resolution and achieves a better accuracy-efficiency trade-off, demonstrating the effectiveness of on-demand visual evidence retrieval for fine-grained perception.

</details>


### [25] [Beyond Visual Boundaries: Rethinking Scene Segmentation for Movie RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28699)
*Dong-Hee Kim,Seonwoo Choi,Changbeen Kim,Jungmyung Wi,Juyeon Ko,Youngju Choi,Il Hyeon Mun,Hyunwoo J. Kim,Donghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了RAG在电影理解中的应用，指出现有场景分割方法作为检索单元时效果不如均匀时间分块，并提出叙事中心的大规模场景分割数据集NarraScene来解决这一差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解对多模态大语言模型是根本性挑战，RAG虽提供折中方案，但其效果取决于检索单元的质量。现有场景分割方法在电影理解下游任务中未能超越简单均匀分块，暴露出场景分割基准标注与实际叙事需求之间的错配。

Method: 首先对现有场景分割方法在下游电影理解任务中进行全面评估，发现其表现不及均匀时间分块；随后审计标准场景分割基准，揭示其标注偏向视觉显著性转换而非叙事事件结构；最后提出NarraScene数据集，采用三层认知分类法（物理变化、角色变化、叙事变化）进行标注，要求有效边界必须具备叙事层面转变。

Result: 现有场景分割方法在下游电影理解任务中一致地未超越朴素均匀时间分块；NarraScene作为检索单元时，其叙事驱动的分段优于均匀分块，显著提升了电影理解任务的表现。

Conclusion: 电影RAG中场景分割的核心挑战不在于检测边界，而在于识别对电影理解真正重要的叙事事件单元。叙事中心的数据集和标注范式是提升长视频理解的关键方向。

Abstract: Understanding long-form video remains a fundamental challenge for multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Sparse frame sampling fails to capture fine-grained visual details, while dense sampling quickly exceeds context length limits. Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) offers a promising middle ground by selectively retrieving relevant video segments for grounded generation, yet its effectiveness critically depends on the quality of the video segments used as retrieval units. In this paper, we investigate RAG for movie understanding, which demands story-level reasoning over characters, events, and narrative arcs spanning hours of content. Scene segmentation, a long-studied problem that partitions movies into semantically coherent units, is a natural candidate for defining such retrieval units. We reexamine whether existing methods actually serve this role through comprehensive evaluation on downstream movie understanding tasks, and find that they consistently fail to outperform naive uniform temporal chunking. Our audit of the most standard scene segmentation benchmarks reveals why: current annotations prioritize visually salient transitions over narrative event structure. Motivated by this mismatch, we introduce NarraScene, a narrative-centric scene segmentation dataset annotated with a three-level cognitive taxonomy spanning physical, character, and narrative change, where every valid boundary requires a narrative-level shift. When used as retrieval units, these narrative-grounded segments outperform uniform chunking on downstream movie understanding tasks, suggesting that the central challenge for scene segmentation in movie RAG is not detecting boundaries, but identifying the narrative event units that matter for movie understanding.

</details>


### [26] [TopoAgent: A Structure-Aware Perception-to-Reasoning Framework for Diagram-to-Graph Topology Extraction with Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28701)
*Bangwei Guo,Xujiang Zhao,Yanchi Liu,Wei Cheng,Shengyu Chen,Dongyue Li,Masaharu Morimoto,Takayuki Kuroda,Dimitris Metaxas,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了TopoBench-180基准数据集和TopoAgent框架，用于解决从结构图（diagram）中提取实体及连接关系图谱（topology extraction）的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从结构图中提取拓扑图（实体及其连接关系）对现有视觉-语言模型而言仍具挑战性，因为该任务需要细粒度的感知定位和具备全局一致性的拓扑感知推理。

Method: TopoAgent采用"感知到推理"的结构感知框架，通过组合接地感知、全局结构先验、规范节点清单构建、以节点为中心的局部到全局关系推理以及拓扑一致性约束，渐进式地提取目标图。TopoBench-180包含180个人工验证的结构图，覆盖Web风格和网络风格两类，并配有规范的图标注。

Result: 在TopoBench-180上的实验表明，TopoAgent在边提取等任务上显著优于强视觉-语言模型基线和最近的视觉推理框架。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了多模态结构化理解中的重要空白，建立了图到图拓扑提取的基准和框架，并将公开发布相关资源。

Abstract: Diagram-to-graph topology extraction aims to extract a graph of entities and their connections from a structural diagram. This task remains challenging for current vision-language models because it requires both fine-grained perceptual grounding and topology-aware reasoning with global consistency. We present TopoBench-180, a human-verified benchmark for diagram-to-graph topology extraction, and TopoAgent, a structure-aware perception-to-reasoning framework for reliable topology extraction using large vision-language models. TopoBench-180 contains 180 structural diagrams spanning Web-style and Network-style categories, paired with canonical graph annotations. TopoAgent progressively extracts the target graph by combining grounded perception, global structural priors, canonical node inventory construction, node-centric local-to-global relation reasoning, and topological consistency enforcement. Experiments on TopoBench-180 show that TopoAgent outperforms strong vision-language model baselines and recent visual reasoning frameworks, especially on edge extraction. More broadly, this work fills an important gap in multimodal structured understanding by establishing a benchmark and framework for diagram-to-graph topology extraction. The benchmark and associated resources will be publicly released at https://huggingface.co/datasets/WayneGuo0011/TopoBench-180.

</details>


### [27] [Stochastic Liquid Deformation Fields: An SDE Generalisation of Closed-Form Continuous-Time Cells for Dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28702)
*Mingzhao Li,Arghya Pal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究将可变形3D高斯泼溅（D-3DGS）中的MLP替换为闭式连续时间（CfC）细胞堆栈，并在每个CfC细胞的时间门控上添加高斯扰动，将确定性场转化为随机微分方程（SDE）场。噪声仅在训练时使用，在合成D-NeRF场景上与确定性CfC持平并优于MLP基线，但在真实NeRF-DS场景上确定性版本已最优，添加噪声并无帮助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CfC闭式解仅是噪声驱动系统的确定性极限，丢弃了通常被认为赋予液体网络鲁棒性的随机项。作者希望恢复这一随机项，以探究噪声对动态场景重建效果的影响。

Method: 在可变形3D高斯泼溅的变形场中，将MLP替换为堆叠的CfC细胞，并在每个CfC细胞的时间门控上添加小幅高斯扰动，将确定性变形场转化为随机微分方程（SDE）场。噪声仅在训练阶段使用，无需额外求解器，关闭噪声时精确还原为CfC。

Result: 在合成D-NeRF场景上，随机SDE场与确定性CfC性能持平，且在大多数场景上优于MLP基线；在真实NeRF-DS场景上，确定性CfC极限已是最优结果，添加噪声并无改善。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种将CfC场解读为SDE的清晰方法，并诚实报告了噪声项在何种情况下有帮助、何种情况下无帮助。

Abstract: Deformable 3D Gaussian Splatting (D-3DGS) reconstructs dynamic scenes by deforming a canonical set of 3D Gaussians through a deformation field of frame time. Replacing its MLP with a stack of Closed-form Continuous-time (CfC) cells-a Liquid Neural Network that solves the Liquid Timeconstant ODE in closed form-gives the field continuous-time behaviour at feed-forward cost. That closed form, however, is only the deterministic limit of a noise-driven system, and drops the stochastic term usually credited for the robustness of liquid networks. We put it back: a small Gaussian perturbation is added to the time gate of every CfC cell, turning the deterministic field into a simple stochastic (SDE) one. The noise is used only during training, needs no solver, and reduces exactly to the CfC when switched off. On the synthetic D-NeRF scenes the stochastic field is on par with the deterministic CfC and beats the MLP baseline on most scenes; on the real-world NeRF-DS scenes the deterministic limit is already best and adding noise does not help. The study thus gives both a clean way to read the CfC field as an SDE and an honest account of when a plain noise term helps and when it does not.

</details>


### [28] [Variable-Granularity Tokenization for High-Resolution Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28706)
*Khayrul Islam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VGTok，一种无需训练的tokenizer，通过在编码器之前基于像素的多尺度形态学top-hat分离度对图像区域进行打分，并以每图百分位阈值固定token预算，配合结构张量门控（λ_min）细化区分二维目标结构与一维杂波，实现航空图像检测中的高效token分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原生分辨率的航空检测器面临小目标物体（仅几个像素）与计算/内存限制之间的矛盾。现有ViT检测器在训练前固定统一token网格，无法在保持小目标分辨率的同时有效控制计算开销。

Method: VGTok是一种训练无关的tokenizer：(1) 通过多尺度形态学top-hat对每个区域与周围环境的可分离性打分；(2) 以每图百分位阈值固定token预算；(3) 使用结构张量门控（λ_min）仅在存在二维目标结构处细化处理，一维杂波保持粗粒度；(4) 生成的token集合是图像的严格划分，可直接嵌入Co-DETR + EVA-02 ViT-L检测器中。

Result: 在VisDrone-val上，VGTok在40%-100% token预算下均超越所有已发表结果：40%预算达到44.22 AP（丢弃3/5序列），密集模式达到48.38 AP（超最强已发表结果6.08）。迁移至AI-TOD-v2无需调整，达到37.27 AP和19.51 AP_vt的新SOTA。作为冻结检查点的即插即用模块，以78.5% token达到36.29 AP，超过所有已发表方法，376.3M参数的检测器胜过3.0B多专家模型。编码器计算减少3.1倍，内存减少1.9倍。

Conclusion: 在骨干网络之前仅基于局部可分离性和结构几何固定token预算，可以在主导航空检测的微小目标场景下保持精度，同时大幅降低编码器的计算和内存开销。

Abstract: ViT detectors fix a uniform token grid before any learned stage. A native-resolution aerial detector must then choose between resolving few-pixel objects and staying inside compute and memory limits. We introduce VGTok, a training-free tokenizer that sets patch granularity per region from pixels, ahead of the encoder. VGTok scores each region by multi-scale morphological top-hat separability from its surround, then thresholds those scores at a per-image percentile, which fixes the token budget. A structure-tensor gate ($λ_{\min}$) refines only where two-dimensional object structure supports it, leaving one-dimensional clutter coarse. The resulting token set is a strict partition of the image. In a Co-DETR detector with an EVA-02 ViT-L encoder, VGTok clears every published VisDrone-val AP and AP$_S$ at every budget from 40\% to 100\% of tokens. At 40\% it records 44.22 AP with three fifths of the sequence discarded before the first transformer block; dense, it reaches 48.38 AP, $6.08$ above the strongest published entry. VGTok transfers to AI-TOD-v2 untouched, same scorer and same rank, and sets a new state of the art at 37.27 AP and 19.51 AP$_{vt}$. As a pure drop-in into a frozen checkpoint it reaches 36.29 AP at 78.5\% of tokens, above every published entry, where our 376.3M-parameter detector clears a 3.0B multi-expert model. We show that a token budget fixed before the backbone, from local separability and structure geometry alone, holds accuracy on the tiny-object regimes that dominate aerial detection, at $3.1\times$ less encoder compute and $1.9\times$ less encoder memory. Code and models are available at \href{https://github.com/khayrulbuet13/vgtok}{\texttt{github.com/khayrulbuet13/vgtok}} and \href{https://huggingface.co/khayrulbuet13/vgtok}{\texttt{huggingface.co/khayrulbuet13/vgtok}}.

</details>


### [29] [MANTLE: A Framework for Adaptive In-Situ Planetary Perception Using a Modular Uplink Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28724)
*Pranav Durai,Gary Doran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MANTLE，一个基于共享DINOv2骨干网络的多任务自适应框架，用于火星地形与地貌提取，包含地形分类头（准确率92.56%）和巨石分割头（IoU 0.753），并提出“模块化上行链路原则”（Modular Uplink Principle），使探测器只需在轨保留冻结骨干网络，新能力可在地面训练后轻量上行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行星表面探测任务日益依赖自主机器人理解复杂地形以确保安全导航和科学目标，而地形分类和巨石分割是关键感知能力。现有方法缺乏可扩展的框架，难以在任务生命周期内持续增加新能力。

Method: 采用共享DINOv2骨干提取高层特征，搭配两个任务特定头：分类头用于大尺度地形分类，分割头用于逐像素巨石定位。分别使用HiRISE轨道影像和MSL地表影像构建数据集训练。核心创新是模块化上行链路原则——仅共享冻结骨干需留在航天器上，任务头在地面训练后上行部署。

Result: 地形分类头在七类火星地形上测试准确率达92.56%；巨石分割头验证IoU为0.753，且在未见过的探测器行驶路线测试集上展现出强跨日泛化能力。

Conclusion: MANTLE验证了模块化可扩展框架的可行性，探测器无需一次性具备全部能力，可在任务周期内通过地上训练与上行持续学习和成长，为未来行星探索任务提供了新范式。

Abstract: Planetary surface exploration missions rely increasingly on autonomous robotic platforms capable of interpreting complex terrain to ensure safe navigation, enable targeted science, and improve operational efficiency, as demonstrated across past Mars missions from Viking through Perseverance. Among the key perception capabilities, landform classification provides contextual information for landing site selection and scientific analysis, while boulder segmentation supports hazard assessment and path planning. This paper presents MANTLE, a multi-task adaptive network for terrain and landform extraction. The model uses a shared DINOv2 backbone for high-level feature extraction with task-specific heads: a classification head for large-scale landform classification, and a segmentation head for pixel-wise boulder localization, each trained on curated datasets built respectively from HiRISE orbital imagery and MSL surface-level imagery. The classification head achieved a test accuracy of 92.56% across seven Martian terrain classes, while the segmentation head achieved a validation IoU of 0.753 and showed strong cross-sol generalization on a held-out test set from previously unseen rover traverses. A key advantage of MANTLE is its modular, extensible design, formalized here as the Modular Uplink Principle: only a shared, frozen backbone needs to remain onboard, while subsequent perception capabilities are trained on Earth as lightweight task-specific heads and uplinked without retraining the full model. This work demonstrates two such high-impact capabilities, terrain classification and boulder segmentation, as an initial realization of a framework built to support many more over a mission's lifetime. With this foundation, future explorers need not arrive on Mars fully formed, but can continue to learn, adapt, and grow more capable with every uplink.

</details>


### [30] [Inter-3D VQA: A Roadside Multimodal Benchmark for 3D Spatiotemporally Grounded Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28762)
*Shaozu Ding,Linan Song,Dajiang Suo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Inter-3D VQA，一个大规模路侧多模态基准，用于交叉口场景下基于3D时空的视觉问答，包含407K个QA对及配套的Inter-Geo基线模型和Inter-Metrics评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VQA和MLLM基准主要基于自我车辆视角或2D路侧视频构建，无法充分评估3D空间推理能力，尤其是在真实世界距离、轨迹、基础设施拓扑和交通安全关键交互方面存在明显局限。

Method: 基于同步点云和多视角图像构建路侧基准；提出Inter-Geo MLLM基线，将对象级和场景级对齐的LiDAR表示集成到多模态大语言模型中；提出Inter-Metrics统一评估框架，涵盖文本一致性、数值准确性和语义正确性三个维度。

Result: Inter-Geo在空间和时间推理任务上优于基于图像的VLM模型；Inter-3D VQA包含407K QA对，覆盖车道级位置、物体关系、运动模式和近碰撞交互推理。

Conclusion: Inter-3D VQA为路侧3D时空接地VQA提供了大规模基准，Inter-Geo展示了LiDAR与图像多模态融合在交叉口推理任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Recent advances in visual question answering (VQA) and multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have enabled natural-language reasoning over traffic scenes. However, existing benchmarks are largely built from ego-vehicle views or 2D roadside videos, limiting their ability to evaluate 3D-grounded reasoning over real-world distances, trajectories, infrastructure topology, and safety-critical interactions. We introduce Inter-3D VQA, a large-scale roadside multimodal benchmark for 3D spatiotemporally grounded VQA at intersections. Built from synchronized point clouds and multi-view images, Inter-3D VQA contains 407K QA pairs covering lane-level positions, object relationships, motion patterns, and near-miss-oriented interaction reasoning. We further propose Inter-Geo, an MLLM baseline that integrates object- and scene-level aligned LiDAR representations, and Inter-Metrics, a unified evaluation framework for textual consistency, numerical accuracy, and semantic correctness. Experiments show that Inter-Geo outperforms image-based VLMs, especially on grounded spatial and temporal reasoning tasks. Our benchmark and codes are available at https://github.com/ASU-Suo-Lab/Inter-3D-VQA .

</details>


### [31] [FairReL: Deepfake Detection using Fairness-Aware Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28777)
*Xiaoman Lu,Jiaqi Li,Shuntian Zheng,Huiping Chen,Yu Guan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FairReL，一个针对深度伪造检测中人口统计子组公平性的表示学习框架。通过SVD分解基础模型骨干网络隔离微调诱导的残差表示，并引入两种互补损失——组条件小波去相关（GCWD）和子空间局部均值对齐（SLMA），在四个基准数据集上相比SOTA公平感知检测器将未见数据集AUC提升3.9%，同时将子组FPR差距降低10.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测器在不同人口统计子组上的错误分布不均，即某些群体的真实人脸更容易被误判为伪造。已有的公平感知检测器通常对整个特征表示进行正则化，无法识别或控制导致不公平预测的特定组件，这种粗粒度的干预会过度抑制有价值的伪造线索，同时留下子组人口统计结构。因此需要针对性地识别并控制产生不公平的具体特征组件。

Method: FairReL识别两类子组敏感组件：多尺度空间特征（编码局部面部和伪造模式）和微调诱导的残差特征（使骨干网络适应不公平的训练分布）。框架采用SVD分解的基础模型骨干网络来隔离微调诱导的残差表示，并提出两种互补损失函数：（1）组条件小波去相关（GCWD），在空间小波子带中抑制子组不平衡结构；（2）子空间局部均值对齐（SLMA），在残差表示中将每个真实/伪造类别内的子组均值对齐。

Result: 在FF++、Celeb-DF、DFD和DFDC四个基准数据集上的实验表明，与最先进的公平感知检测器相比，FairReL将未见数据集的AUC提高了3.9%，同时将子组FPR差距降低了10.2%。

Conclusion: FairReL通过针对性地控制子组敏感的特征组件而非整体特征正则化，能够同时提升深度伪造检测的准确率与跨人口统计子组的公平性，验证了组件级公平干预策略的有效性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Although recent deepfake detectors achieve high overall accuracy, their errors remain unevenly distributed across demographic subgroups, with real faces from certain groups more often misclassified as fake. Existing fairness-aware detectors typically regularise the entire feature representation, without identifying or controlling the specific components that drive unfair predictions. Such coarse intervention can over-suppress useful forgery cues while leaving demographic structure in component-specific subspaces. To address this, we identify two subgroup-sensitive components: multi-scale spatial features, which encode local facial and forgery patterns, and fine-tuning-induced residual features, which adapt the backbone to the unfair training distribution. We propose FairReL, a fairness-aware representation-learning framework that targets both components with dedicated demographic supervision. FairReL uses an SVD-decomposed foundation-model backbone to isolate the fine-tuning-induced residual representation, and introduces two complementary losses. Group-Conditional Wavelet Decorrelation (GCWD) suppresses subgroup-imbalanced structure across spatial wavelet sub-bands, while Subspace-Localised Mean Alignment (SLMA) aligns subgroup means within each real/fake class in the residual representation. Experiments on FF++, Celeb-DF, DFD and DFDC show that, against the state-of-the-art fairness-aware detector, FairReL improves unseen-dataset AUC by 3.9% while reducing subgroup FPR disparity by 10.2%. Code is available at https://github.com/xiaoman89/FairReL .

</details>


### [32] [ClearText-Video: A Large-Scale Text-Centric Video Dataset Bridging Video Restoration and Scene-Text Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28784)
*Jinlong Li,Jiaming Ding,Dingfu Lu,Malcolm Hsiu,Chuang Ke,Kangning Yang,Bochen Guan,Lan Fu,Jie Cai,Huiming Sun,Zibo Meng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ClearText-Video (CTVid)，一个大规模、场景文本感知的视频理解基准，包含4,639个真实世界文本丰富的第一人称视频、550K+帧、160万人工验证的场景文本标注及22万+中英文空间/时间问答对，并针对每个高质量视频提供内容匹配的降质和恢复变体，用于系统研究多模态大语言模型在不同视频质量条件下的文本阅读与推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在视觉-语言理解上取得显著进展，但其在文本中心视频推理中对输入质量高度敏感。真实用户提供的视频常含运动模糊、压缩伪影、噪声和低分辨率文本，这会损害可靠文本读取和下游推理。是否存在能够鲁棒地阅读和推理真实场景文本的MLLMs仍是一个基础开放问题，因此需要系统化的基准来研究这一问题。

Method: 构建CTVid基准：包含4,639个真实世界文本丰富的第一人称视频、550K+帧、160万人工验证场景文本标注、22万+中英文问答对。为每个高质量视频生成内容匹配的降质和恢复变体，支持两个任务族：文本中心视频恢复和多质量视频问答。评估18种代表性的恢复方法和16个最先进的MLLMs。

Result: 实验结果显示：视觉增强并不能保证文本保真度或下游推理能力的提升；模糊比低分辨率更具破坏性；恢复视频可能改变MLLMs使用的文本证据；仅依赖OCR的流水线远低于直接的多模态推理性能。

Conclusion: CTVid揭示了视频恢复与文本基础理解之间的差距，为构建恢复感知、质量鲁棒的文本中心视频系统提供了严格的基础。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have recently made strong progress in visual--linguistic understanding. However, their performance on text-centric video reasoning remains highly sensitive to input quality. Real-world user-provided videos often contain motion blur, compression artifacts, noise, and low-resolution text, which impair reliable text reading and downstream reasoning. Whether MLLMs can robustly read and reason about real-world scene text under diverse quality conditions remains a fundamental open question. We introduce ClearText-Video (CTVid), a large-scale, scene-text-aware benchmark for studying text-centric video understanding under controlled quality variation. CTVid contains 4,639 real-world text-rich egocentric videos, 550K+ frames, 1.6M human-verified scene-text annotations, and 220K+ spatial/temporal question--answer pairs in Chinese and English. For each high-quality video, CTVid provides content-matched Degraded-Quality and Restored-Quality variants, supporting two task families: Text-Centric Video Restoration and Multi-Quality VideoQA. We evaluate 18 representative restoration methods and 16 state-of-the-art MLLMs on CTVid. The results show that visual enhancement does not guarantee textual fidelity or downstream reasoning gains: blur is more damaging than low resolution, restored videos can alter the textual evidence used by MLLMs, and OCR-only pipelines remain far below direct multimodal reasoning. CTVid exposes the gap between video restoration and text-grounded understanding, providing a rigorous foundation for restoration-aware, quality-robust text-centric video systems.

</details>


### [33] [Beyond Representation Learning: A Systematic Study of Joint-Embedding Predictive Generation for 3D Brain MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28787)
*Meng Zhou,Wenhao You,Yuxing Chen,Yueying Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Med-D-JEPA，将联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）从自监督表征学习扩展到3D医学图像生成领域。该方法在3D KL正则化对抗变分自编码器的连续潜变量token上，结合掩码上下文预测、表征级对齐、逐token扩散和迭代next-set-of-token采样，在BraTS2019、OASIS-1和BraTS2020数据集上实现了优于或匹敌多个强基线的生成质量，并显著提升了下游分类和分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合嵌入预测架构（JEPAs）主要应用于自监督表征学习，而Denoising JEPA（D-JEPA）虽在自然图像生成上展现了强大能力，但尚未探索其在3D医学影像领域的适用性。作者旨在填补这一空白，系统性地将JEPA范式引入3D脑部MRI生成。

Method: Med-D-JEPA基于D-JEPA框架，在3D KL正则化对抗变分自编码器产生的连续潜变量token上运行。核心组件包括：掩码上下文预测、表征级对齐、逐token扩散生成以及迭代next-set-of-token采样策略。评估涵盖无条件与类别条件生成（BraTS2019、OASIS-1）、下游分类任务以及BraTS2020上的全肿瘤分割预训练。

Result: 在多个生成设置下，Med-D-JEPA在保真度和多样性指标上优于或匹敌多个强基线。在分类效用方面，基于Med-D-JEPA合成数据的预训练将BraTS2019的分类AUC从0.63提升至0.85，OASIS-1从0.78提升至0.87；在分割研究中，预训练将Dice从0.74提升至0.80，HD95从13.40mm降至9.56mm。

Conclusion: 联合嵌入预测生成范式在3D医学图像合成方面展现出了巨大潜力，Med-D-JEPA不仅在生成质量上达到甚至超越现有方法，其合成数据还能有效提升下游分类和分割任务性能，为该方向的研究提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) have primarily been developed for self-supervised representation learning. Denoising JEPA (D-JEPA) recently demonstrated strong generative capabilities on natural images, yet the applicability to 3D medical imaging remains unexplored. Building on the D-JEPA framework, we present Med-D-JEPA, a systematic adaptation and evaluation of joint-embedding predictive generation for 3D brain MRI. Med-D-JEPA operates on continuous latent tokens produced by a 3D KL-regularized adversarial variational autoencoder, and combines masked context prediction, representation-level alignment, per-token diffusion, and iterative next-set-of-token sampling. We evaluate unconditional and class-conditional generation quality on BraTS2019 and OASIS-1 datasets; downstream classification utility; and preliminary whole-tumor segmentation on BraTS2020. Across different generation settings, Med-D-JEPA achieves superior or competitive performance compared to several strong baselines on fidelity and diversity metrics. Compared to training with real samples, Med-D-JEPA-based synthetic pretraining improves classification AUC from 0.63 to 0.85 on BraTS2019 and from 0.78 to 0.87 on OASIS-1. In the segmentation study, pretraining on Med-D-JEPA samples improves Dice from 0.74 to 0.80 and reduces HD95 from 13.40 to 9.56 mm. These findings establish joint-embedding predictive generation as a promising direction for 3D medical image synthesis and encourage further research in this direction.

</details>


### [34] [Blind Stereoscopic Omnidirectional Image Quality Assessment Using Predictive Coding Hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28798)
*Wei Zhou,André Kaup*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于预测编码层级启发（PCH）的无参考立体全向图像质量评估方法，通过模拟人类视觉系统的局部环视感知和全局预测感知过程，实现对视口采样、环视转换、显著性检测、双目融合预测编码等环节的建模，最终在多个数据集上取得了优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 立体全向图像（SOI）在虚拟现实中提供了沉浸式体验，但其质量评估面临自由视场角和双目视觉等多重挑战，现有方法难以高效准确地评估SOI的感知质量。

Method: 基于人类视觉系统特性，提出预测编码层级启发（PCH）指标，包含三个模块：1）局部环视感知模块——提取不同视口，进行环视转换和显著性检测，模拟人脑的感知与注意力；2）全局预测感知模块——基于双目机制融合左右视图，进行预测编码层级建模；3）视觉质量回归器——结合局部与全局感知线索得到最终质量分数。

Result: 大量实验表明，所提出的PCH方法在多个数据集上与最先进的质量评估方法相比，取得了具有竞争力和持续改进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过模拟观看SOIs的局部与全局信息处理流程，PCH指标有效提升了无参考立体全向图像质量评估的准确性，为VR环境下的体验质量度量提供了新思路。

Abstract: Stereoscopic omnidirectional images (SOIs) have provided users with newly immersive quality of experience in virtual reality environments. However, developing efficient and accurate perceptual quality assessment metrics for SOIs remains challenging due to many factors such as freely changeable field of views and binocular vision. In this paper, based on the characteristics of the human visual system (HVS), we propose a Predictive Coding Hierarchy-inspired metric (PCH) for blind/no-reference stereoscopic omnidirectional image quality assessment. Motivated by the viewing process of SOIs, the proposed PCH includes a local cyclopean perception module, a global predictive perception module, and a visual quality regressor. First, observers browse different spherical sceneries from viewports, and aggregate the local visual information to infer the perceptual quality of SOIs. Therefore, we extract various viewports, followed by cyclopean conversion and saliency detection to approach the perception and attention of the human brain. After the local aggregation, viewers then infer the global scene in their minds. Based on the binocular mechanism, we fuse left and right views to perform predictive coding hierarchy modelling. Finally, the visual quality regressor is exploited to obtain the ultimate quality score related to both local and global perceptual cues. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed PCH achieves competitive and consistently improved performance compared with state-of-the-art quality assessment methods.

</details>


### [35] [A Large-scale Evaluation of Text-guided Models for Facial Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28802)
*Rahul Nair,Saurav Pandit,Hannah Kerner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个大规模（约100万张图像）的文本引导扩散模型面部编辑评估基准，包含169个面部编辑属性的数据集Face-Edit-Attributes，系统评估了6个流行模型在顺序面部编辑任务上的表现，发现大多数模型在头发和配饰编辑上表现良好但在姿态编辑上表现不佳，且所有模型都存在过度编辑问题，并揭示了人口统计学偏差（深肤色男性和老年人面孔更容易被过度编辑）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然文本引导扩散模型已在全场景编辑中被广泛测试，但尚未被全面评估用于面部编辑任务。现有方法（GAN和3DMM）各有局限：GAN编辑多样但不稳定，3DMM稳定但只能修改姿态和表情。因此需要系统评估文本引导模型在面部编辑中的能力。

Method: 构建了Face-Edit-Attributes数据集，包含169个面部编辑属性（聚焦头发、配饰和姿态编辑）。在CelebA和CelebSET两个流行名人数据集上，对6个文本引导扩散模型进行了约100万张图像的顺序面部编辑任务大规模评估。此外还评估了各模型的人口统计学偏差。

Result: 大多数模型在头发和配饰编辑上表现良好，但在姿态编辑方面表现困难。所有模型都存在过度编辑问题（例如仅要求改变发型时也改变了发色）。几乎所有模型对深肤色男性面孔和老年人面孔产生了更多的过度编辑，揭示了令人惊讶的偏差。

Conclusion: 文本引导扩散模型虽有潜力成为面部编辑的有力工具，但在姿态编辑和过度编辑控制方面仍需改进，且需要关注和缓解人口统计学偏差。作者公开了代码、数据和约100万张图像的数据集以供后续研究。

Abstract: Facial appearance editing powers popular applications like FaceApp and Photoshop. Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs) and 3D Morphable Models (3DMMs) have been widely used for facial editing. GANs can perform varied facial edits (e.g., changing hair color, hairstyle), but often produce unstable edits. 3DMMs produce stable edits, but can only alter pose and facial expression. Recently, text-guided diffusion models like Nano Banana have become popular for image editing. Text-guided models are a compelling alternative to GANs and 3DMMs since they can produce both stable and varied image edits. While text-guided models have been widely tested for whole-scene edits (e.g., ``make the woman play a guitar''), they have not been comprehensively tested for facial editing. We conducted the first large-scale evaluation ($\sim1$M images evaluated) of six popular text-guided models on a sequential facial editing task. We present Face-Edit-Attributes, the largest collection of $169$ facial editing attributes focused on hair, accessories, and pose edits. We compared model performance using two popular celebrity face datasets: CelebA and CelebSET. Our results show that most models performed hair and accessory edits well, but struggled with editing pose. All models over-edit (e.g., changing hair color when asked only to change the hairstyle). We also evaluated demographic biases in each model. Our results show surprising biases in overediting: almost all models created more overedits for dark-skinned male faces and old faces. The code and data for our results (including our repository of $\sim 1$M images) can be accessed \href{https://github.com/rahul1801/Face-Edit-Bench}{\textcolor{blue}{here}}.

</details>


### [36] [FigMirror: Ground It, Code It, Plot It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28814)
*Xiaohan Zhao,Jiacheng Liu,Yaxin Luo,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FigMirror，一个基于计算机使用模型的代理框架，通过坐标定位和可执行代码测量来实现科学图表的参考风格迁移（将新数据以参考图的视觉风格绘制），并构建了专家标注的PlotTwin-Bench基准进行评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要聚焦于复现参考图本身，而实际应用中更常见的是在保留参考图视觉风格（如配色和排版）的前提下绘制新数据。已有方法通过像素级优化模仿参考图，难以将风格迁移到新数据上。

Method: 提出FigMirror代理框架，利用现代计算机使用模型中的坐标定位和编码能力，通过Grounded Measurement（基于坐标定位视觉元素并通过可执行代码测量其属性）来实现风格迁移，并构建PlotTwin-Bench基准（含细粒度代码和图像级风格指标）。

Result: 实验表明FigMirror在参考条件风格迁移任务上 consistently 优于现有方法。论文中所有图表（除对比方法生成的之外）均由FigMirror生成。

Conclusion: 坐标定位与编码能力是参考图风格迁移的关键，代理框架FigMirror有效解锁了这些能力，为科学图表风格迁移提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Converting scientific figures into executable code has gained increasing attention, yet existing methods primarily focus on reproducing the reference figure itself. A more practical setting is to plot new data while preserving the visual style of a reference figure (e.g., color scheme and typography). Prior approaches mimic the reference through pixel-level optimization and struggle to carry its style to new data. We show that the key to this task lies in the coordinate grounding and coding capabilities present in modern computer-use models. We propose FigMirror, an agentic framework that unlocks these capabilities through Grounded Measurement, which locates visual elements by coordinates and measures their properties through executable code. We further introduce PlotTwin-Bench, an expert-curated benchmark with fine-grained code and image-level style metrics. Experiments show that FigMirror consistently outperforms existing methods on reference-conditioned style transfer. All plots in this paper are generated by FigMirror, except those produced by other methods for comparison. Our code and data are available at: https://github.com/VILA-Lab/FigMirror.

</details>


### [37] [Text-Driven Artistic Staging: Pose, Lighting, and Camera References from Paintings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28823)
*Yunge Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的任务——文本到可编辑3D场景布局（text-to-editable 3D staging），从情感描述中联合生成人体姿势、主光源和相机配置。作者从2,328幅具象绘画中构建了11,911个文本-场景布局配对数据，并训练了一个流匹配变换器（flow-matching transformer）来支持可变数量的角色并生成多个场景布局备选方案。在保留数据集多样性的同时，模型在检索R@1指标上达到32.2%，显著优于基于CLIP的最近邻检索（16.6%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成方法通常将人体姿势、光照和相机配置作为独立元素分别建模，忽略了艺术家在创作时是通过协调这些要素共同传达叙事和情感的。本文旨在打破这一局限，提出联合生成这三个要素的新任务。

Method: 从2,328幅具象绘画中重建SMPL人体模型、估计低频光照、恢复相机参数，并与ArtEmis情感描述配对构建数据集。设计了一个流匹配变换器（flow-matching transformer），能够处理可变数量的角色并为每个提示生成多个场景布局方案。

Result: 在保留的测试描述上，模型实现了32.2%的检索R@1，显著优于基于CLIP最近邻检索的16.6%，同时大致保持了语料库级别的多样性。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明了从文本生成可编辑、情感条件化的3D场景布局参考的可行性，为该方向的研究提供了新的任务设定和基准。

Abstract: Artists coordinate human pose, illumination, and camera placement to convey narrative and emotion, but existing generative methods typically model these elements independently. We introduce text-to-editable 3D staging, a task that jointly generates human poses, a dominant light, and a camera configuration from an affective description. We construct 11,911 text--staging pairs from 2,328 figurative paintings by reconstructing SMPL bodies, estimating low-frequency illumination, recovering camera parameters, and pairing each scene with ArtEmis descriptions. We train a flow-matching transformer that supports variable numbers of figures and produces multiple staging alternatives for each prompt. On held-out descriptions, the model achieves 32.2\% retrieval R@1, compared with 16.6\% for CLIP-based nearest-neighbor retrieval, while approximately preserving corpus-level diversity. These results demonstrate the feasibility of generating editable, emotionally conditioned 3D staging references from text.

</details>

*James Pritts,Till Sittart,Hendrik Sauer,Silja Janßen,Felix Seegräber,David Nakath,Kevin Köser*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BlobBoards，一种基于密集多尺度高斯斑点场的基准标记系统，通过特征匹配实现联合检测、识别和位姿估计，在检测率、位姿精度和遮挡鲁棒性方面均优于AprilTag和ArUco等现有系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准标记系统（如AprilTag、ArUco）在小型标记、遮挡和极端视角条件下检测率和位姿精度不足，需要一种更鲁棒的标记系统来应对实际应用中的复杂场景。

Method: BlobBoards由密集多尺度高斯斑点场构成，利用数百个斑点特征的空间覆盖约束位姿，多尺度特性保证在不同焦距、距离和倾斜角度下的可检测性；通过学习型局部描述子与参考模式匹配并进行空间验证，从而确定对应关系、位姿和身份。

Result: 与动捕真值对比，BlobBoards的中位平移误差为3.6-5.0mm，在小板上比AprilTag降低89%，大板上降低70%；检测率达80%（AprilTag 74%，ArUco 58%）；在50%遮挡下仍检测69%的板且精度基本不变，而AprilTag和ArUco完全失效。

Conclusion: BlobBoards在检测率、位姿精度和遮挡鲁棒性三方面均达到当前最优水平，尤其在小型标记和严重遮挡场景下展现出显著优势。

Abstract: We propose BlobBoards, a fiducial marker system comprising a dense, multi-scale field of Gaussian blobs and a feature-based pipeline for joint detection, identification, and pose estimation. Each board is registered from hundreds of blob features whose dense spatial coverage constrains pose, while multiple scales preserve detectability across large changes in focal length, distance, and obliquity. Learned local descriptors are matched to the reference pattern and spatially verified, so the correspondences determine pose and certify identity. Against motion-capture ground truth, BlobBoards achieve median translation errors of 3.6-5.0 mm, reducing AprilTag's median translation error by 89% on small boards and 70% on large ones. They also produce far fewer large-rotation failures than state-of-the-art tag systems. BlobBoards achieve the highest detection rate, 80% versus 74% for AprilTag and 58% for ArUco, with the largest margin on the smallest markers. Under 50% occlusion, they still detect 69% of boards with essentially unchanged median translation error, while AprilTag and ArUco detect none. In experiments BlobBoards give state-of-the-art detection rate, pose accuracy and occlusion robustness.

</details>


### [39] [MWIR-4-Plastic: The Identification of Complex End-of-Life Industrial Plastic using Mid-wave Infrared Hyperspectral Imaging and Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28874)
*Elias Arbash,Andréa de Lima Ribeiro,Filipa Simões,Ahmed Jamal Afifi,Aldino Rizaldy,Yuleika Madriz,Samuel Thiele,Sandra Lorenz,Margret Fuchs,Pedram Ghamisi,Paul Scheunders,Richard Gloaguen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次公开了来自报废车辆中黑色碎塑料的高光谱成像（HSI）数据集，涵盖四种工业聚合物、13个配准的RGB/VNIR/SWIR/MWIR场景，并提出了一个多模态光谱-空间分割与分类框架，通过九种方法建立了首个综合基准，实现复杂黑色塑料的准确分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分选方法依赖单点接触式红外光谱或实验室HSI，缺乏空间分辨分析能力；数据集多为实验室受控环境且针对完整塑料而非碎塑料；黑色工业塑料代表性不足；分类管线依赖人工区域选择和规则匹配，忽略了空间信息和现代深度学习方法的潜力。

Method: 构建了含13个配准场景（RGB、VNIR、SWIR、MWIR）的碎黑塑料HSI数据集；提出多模态光谱-空间框架，包括前景隔离、像素级分类和对象级多数投票；将地球观测领域的高级高光谱Transformer适配引入，并结合化学计量学波段选择。

Result: 建立了首个包含九种处理方法（化学计量学、机器学习和深度学习架构）的综合基准，在复杂黑色塑料分类上取得准确结果；完整数据集和方法公开以保障可复现性。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了碎黑塑料HSI数据集缺失的空白，验证了融合空间信息与深度学习的高光谱分析框架在工业分选中的有效性，为工业检测中的高光谱物体分析管线建立了公开基准。

Abstract: The automated sorting of shredded black plastics from end-of-life (EOF) industrial waste presents a significant challenge in recycling facilities, primarily due to the limitations of current sensing and analytical approaches. Existing studies predominantly rely on single-point contact-based mid-infrared spectroscopy or laboratory hyperspectral imaging (HSI) setups, which fail to provide the spatially resolved analysis necessary for fast, bulk processing. Moreover, available datasets are laboratory-controlled and focus on intact rather than shredded plastics, hindering further recycling refinement. Black industrial plastics, in particular, are underrepresented, while most classification pipelines depend on manual region selection and rule-based spectral matching, neglecting spatial information and modern deep learning (DL) methods. To address these gaps, we introduce the first publicly available HSI dataset of shredded black plastics from EOF vehicle, comprising four industrial polymers across 13 co-registered RGB, VNIR, SWIR, and MWIR scenes and their segmentation pipeline. We developed a multi-modal spectral-spatial framework that integrates foreground isolation, pixel-wise classification, and object-level majority voting. By adapting advanced hyperspectral transformers from earth observation and incorporating chemometric band selection, we achieve accurate classification of complex black plastics. The study establishes the first comprehensive benchmark using nine processing methods, including chemometric, machine learning, and DL architectures. To ensure reproducibility, the complete dataset and methodologies are publicly released, establishing a benchmark for a hyperspectral object-analysis pipeline in industrial inspection.

</details>


### [40] [Pixel-wise Geo-registration of Drone and Satellite Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28891)
*Qingyang Liu,David G Shatwell,Parth Parag Kulkarni,Mubarak Shah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SkyReg数据集和基准，用于像素级无人机到卫星图像的跨视角地理配准，提供密集的逐像素地理监督，并训练了一种几何感知重建管线，取得了显著优于现有方法的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨视角地理定位基准大多仅提供GPS标签，只能评估每张图像单个坐标，缺乏像素级密集地理配准的评估，导致跨视角地理配准这一重要任务尚未被充分探索。

Method: 构建SkyReg数据集，涵盖正射和透视、城市/地标/郊区等多种场景和相机配置，提供密集逐像素地理定位监督；基于该数据集评估检索、特征匹配、单应性对齐和前馈3D重建等多类基线方法，并训练了几何感知的重建管线。

Result: SkyReg上训练的几何感知重建管线在像素级跨视角地理配准中实现了最先进的性能，相比现有基线有显著提升。

Conclusion: SkyReg填补了跨视角地理配准领域密集监督基准的空白，证明了利用该数据集训练的几何感知方法能有效推动像素级地理配准的进步。

Abstract: Pixel-level cross-view geo-registration aims to align a query image (e.g., drone) to a geo-referenced satellite map so that every query pixel can be mapped to real-world GPS coordinates. Despite strong progress in cross-view geo-localization, existing benchmarks largely provide only GPS labels, limiting evaluation to a single coordinate per image and leaving dense geodetic alignment underexplored. We introduce SkyReg, a dataset and standardized benchmark for pixel-level drone-to-satellite geo-registration, providing dense per-pixel geo-location supervision across diverse settings (orthographic and perspective), scene types (urban, landmark-centric, suburban/rural), and camera configurations. Using SkyReg, we evaluate a broad set of baselines spanning retrieval, feature matching, homography-based alignment, and feed-forward 3D reconstruction. Finally, cross-view pairs from SkyReg, we train a geometry-aware reconstruction pipeline that achieves state-of-the-art results,improving performance by a significant margin.

</details>


### [41] [ReconSplat: Generalizable 3D Scene Reconstruction Beyond Observed Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28895)
*Giuseppe Stracquadanio,Kevin Raj,Julia Grabinski,Stefan Roth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReconSplat是一种前馈式3D场景重建模型，结合3D高斯溅射与多视角潜空间扩散模型，同时实现几何一致的视图生成和精确深度估计，在RealEstate10K和DL3DV-10K基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景重建方法一直面临未观察区域视图生成与几何一致性之间的权衡，难以同时保证新视角的合理性和几何对齐的准确性。

Method: 以3DGS作为中间可微场景表示，集成多视角潜空间扩散模型（MV-LDM）作为细化器和修复器；通过前馈3DGS表示编码外观与几何的变分3D潜特征，并将其栅格化到2D潜空间以引导扩散过程，从而保证几何一致性。

Result: 在RealEstate10K和DL3DV-10K真实世界数据集上，ReconSplat在具有挑战性的外推场景中同时生成照片级逼真的新视角和准确的深度图，性能优于现有方法。

Conclusion: ReconSplat成功实现了对未见及困难视角的外推，并且生成连贯、精确的场景几何，有效解决了视图生成与几何一致性之间的长期权衡问题。

Abstract: We introduce ReconSplat, a feed-forward model for 3D scene reconstruction that aims to address the longstanding trade-off between plausible view generation for unobserved regions and geometric consistency, providing both geometrically aligned novel views and sharp depth estimates. Our approach builds on 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) as an intermediate differentiable scene representation and integrates it with a multi-view latent diffusion model (MV-LDM) trained to act simultaneously as a refiner and an inpainter for appearance and scene geometry. We enforce geometric consistency by guiding the diffusion process with variational 3D latent features for appearance and geometry, encoded by the feed-forward 3DGS representation and rasterized to 2D latent space. ReconSplat produces both photorealistic novel views and accurate depth maps on real-world benchmarks, RealEstate10K and DL3DV-10K, outperforming existing methods in challenging extrapolation setups. Notably, ReconSplat allows the extrapolation of unseen and challenging viewpoints jointly with coherent and precise scene geometry.

</details>


### [42] [Coarse to Fine: Iterative Adversarial Neural Cellular Automata for Medical Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28909)
*Anh Thi Luu,Nick Lemke,Anirban Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出StyleGANCA，是首个轻量级通用NCA生成对抗网络，结合StyleGAN风格映射与自适应风格调制到多尺度NCA合成过程中，以更少的参数实现竞争性图像质量，在PathMNIST上以617k参数取得最佳FID和KID分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像领域因隐私和法律限制难以共享数据，合成数据生成提供隐私友好的替代方案。然而现有SOTA生成模型虽能生成高质量图像，但计算开销大，难以在资源受限硬件上应用。

Method: 提出StyleGANCA架构，将StyleGAN启发的映射网络和自适应风格调制集成到多尺度NCA合成过程中，通过迭代局部交互实现潜变量控制的图像生成。

Result: 在BloodMNIST和PathMNIST上与对抗、变分、扩散及NCA基线对比，StyleGANCA以显著更少的参数达到竞争性图像质量，在PathMNIST上以617k参数取得最佳FID与KID分数；下游实验证明生成图像保留类别信息并能有效支持多类分类器训练。

Conclusion: StyleGANCA证明了基于NCA的轻量级GAN架构能在资源受限条件下生成高质量医学图像，为隐私受限场景下的医学影像合成提供可行方案。

Abstract: Large-scale, publicly available datasets have driven advances in deep learning, but privacy and legal restrictions often limit data sharing in medical imaging. Synthetic data generation offers a privacy-friendly alternative to enable the training of high-performance models on health data. While most state-of-the-art generative models produce high-quality images, they remain computationally expensive, which limits their applicability on resource-constrained hardware. We propose StyleGANCA, the first lightweight general-purpose NCA-based generative adversarial network. The architecture integrates a StyleGAN-inspired mapping network and adaptive style modulation into a multi-scale NCA synthesis process, enabling latent-controlled image generation through iterative local interactions. We evaluate StyleGANCA on BloodMNIST and PathMNIST against adversarial, variational, diffusion, and NCA-based baselines. Experimental results demonstrate that StyleGANCA achieves competitive image quality with substantially fewer parameters than baseline architectures, achieving the best FID and KID scores on PathMNIST with only 617k parameters. Furthermore, downstream experiments show that the generated images preserve class-specific information and effectively support the training of multi-class classifiers. Our code is publicly available at: https://github.com/MECLabTUDA/StyleGANCA

</details>


### [43] [mmIR: Frequency-Space Inverse Rendering for 3D Millimeter-Wave Radar ADC Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28913)
*Adnan Armouti,Yixuan Gao,Rajalakshmi Nandakumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了mmIR，一个开源的、可微分的FMCW雷达逆渲染器。它通过将基于物理的前向模型拟合到真实雷达捕获数据，并从密集虚拟孔径重新渲染，从而合成高分辨率3D雷达数据。mmIR利用LiDAR辅助逆渲染方法，在LiDAR网格上优化ITU物理材质参数、顶点法线和天线波束方向图，通过端到端自动微分实现多径传播、极化和自由空间衍射的相位相干MIMO前向建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率3D雷达数据稀缺。商用毫米波传感器天线阵列小导致角分辨率有限（仅几度），现有数据集只有2D距离-方位图或稀疏点云，而非原始ADC信号。硬件扩展成本高，合成孔径扫描在车队规模不切实际，学习合成方法又受限于数据短缺本身。

Method: mmIR采用LiDAR辅助逆渲染范式：先用LiDAR获取场景网格作为几何支架，然后通过端到端自动微分优化逐顶点的ITU物理材质参数、顶点法线和天线波束方向图。其核心是相位相干的MIMO前向模型，支持多径传播、极化和自由空间衍射，可拟合真实采集数据并重新渲染到密集虚拟孔径。

Result: 在7个室外和6个室内ColoRadar场景中，mmIR在距离-方位图上的平均Pearson相关系数达到0.914，远优于Sionna-RT的0.307。级联成像雷达训练的模型可无需再训练直接迁移到同一位置的单芯片雷达（相关性0.554），100x100元素的密集虚拟阵列生成的单帧3D占用率经LiDAR验证有效。

Conclusion: mmIR展示了基于物理的逆渲染方法可以绕过数据稀缺难题，通过可微分的FMCW雷达渲染模型生成高质量的高分辨率3D雷达数据，且具有良好的跨设备迁移能力和大规模数据合成潜力。

Abstract: High-resolution 3D radar data is scarce. Commodity mmWave sensors use small antenna arrays that limit angular resolution to several degrees, and existing datasets provide only 2D range-azimuth maps or sparse point clouds rather than raw analog-to-digital converter (ADC) signals. Hardware scaling is expensive, synthetic-aperture scanning is impractical at fleet scale, and learned synthesis methods are bottlenecked by the very data shortage they aim to address. We present mmIR, an open-source differentiable frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) radar inverse renderer that fits a physics-based forward model to real captures and re-renders from dense virtual apertures to synthesize high-resolution 3D radar data. Because radar resolution is too coarse to recover geometry directly, mmIR performs LiDAR-assisted inverse rendering: using LiDAR-derived meshes as a geometric scaffold, mmIR optimizes per-vertex International Telecommunication Union (ITU) physics materials, vertex normals, and antenna beam patterns through end-to-end automatic differentiation of a phase-coherent multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) forward model with multi-bounce propagation, polarization, and free-space diffraction. On seven outdoor and six indoor ColoRadar scenes, mmIR achieves 0.914 mean Pearson correlation on range-azimuth maps versus 0.307 for Sionna-RT. Scenes trained on a cascaded imaging radar transfer to a co-located single-chip radar without re-training (0.554 correlation), and dense virtual arrays (100x100 elements) produce single-frame 3D occupancy validated against LiDAR. Project page: https://mmwave-inverse-rendering.github.io/

</details>


### [44] [ActiveAugment: Online Active Learning for Augmentation Selection in Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28923)
*Noah Videcrantz,Mostafa Mehdipour Ghazi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ActiveAugment将数据增强策略选择建模为在线主动学习问题，通过模型不确定性和特征差异评分，为每个样本动态选择最脆弱的增强方式，并结合监督分类与监督对比学习目标进行训练，在多个基准数据集上超越AutoAugment、RandAugment和TrivialAugment。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据增强策略要么依赖昂贵的、数据集特定的策略搜索，要么随机均匀应用变换，忽略了模型当前的学习状态，缺乏自适应性。

Method: 将增强选择视为在线主动学习问题：每个训练小批量生成候选增强视图池，用模型预测不确定性和增强引起的特征差异进行评分，选择使当前模型最脆弱的增强方式；训练目标为联合监督分类和监督对比学习，增强类内不变性同时保持类间分离。

Result: 在8个涵盖自然图像和医学影像的基准数据集上，结合CNN和Transformer架构以及三种训练模式，ActiveAugment在受控增强偏移下全面超越AutoAugment、RandAugment和TrivialAugment，尤其在低标注预算和医学影像数据上增益显著。

Conclusion: 增强选择策略应在训练过程中动态演化，且选择策略对泛化性能有直接影响；ActiveAugment展现了强大的跨域适应性，在数据稀缺和大域偏移场景下尤为有效。

Abstract: Data augmentation is a cornerstone of deep learning pipelines, yet existing strategies treat it as a static, model-agnostic preprocessing step, either relying on expensive dataset-specific policy search or applying transformations uniformly at random, regardless of what the model has already learned. We introduce ActiveAugment, a unified framework that treats augmentation selection as an online active learning problem. For each training minibatch, ActiveAugment generates a pool of candidate augmented views and scores each candidate using a combination of the model's predictive uncertainty and the feature discrepancy induced by the augmentation. The augmentation under which the current model is most fragile is selected per sample, and the model is then trained with a joint supervised classification and supervised contrastive objective that enforces intra-class invariance to the selected augmentations while maintaining inter-class separation. We evaluate ActiveAugment on eight benchmark datasets spanning natural and medical imaging, using CNN and transformer architectures across three training regimes (training from scratch, full fine-tuning, and linear probing), and comparing eight active selection strategies for augmentation scoring. ActiveAugment outperforms AutoAugment, RandAugment, and TrivialAugment under controlled augmentation shifts across all domains and budgets, with the most pronounced gains at low labelling budgets. On medical imaging datasets, where data is scarce and domain shift relative to natural-image pretrained models is large, ActiveAugment achieves higher test F1 than all baselines, demonstrating strong cross-domain adaptability. Our analysis reveals that the augmentation selection policy evolves meaningfully during training and that strategy choice has a direct impact on generalisation. Code is available at: https://github.com/noahvide/ActiveAugment.

</details>


### [45] [NBS: No Bias Stereo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28933)
*Vage Taamazyan,Zhuowen Shen,Stefan Hinterstoisser,Alberto Dall'Olio,Agastya Kalra,Aarrushi Shandilya,Xin Li,Wenping Wang,Kartik Venkataraman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文挑战了立体匹配任务中必须依赖架构归纳偏置的传统观点，证明确实可以通过完全无偏置的端到端Vision Transformer模型，在巨大合成数据集上训练，同时实现最先进的精度和更优的运行效率，从而为3D重建解锁真正的扩展法则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 立体匹配是计算机视觉中最后一个所有最先进方法都依赖重架构归纳偏置的任务。尽管已有研究表明通用方法可以解决该任务，但学界普遍认为归纳偏置对高质量结果和计算效率是严格必要的。本文旨在挑战这一范式。

Method: 采用完全去除架构归纳偏置的简单端到端Vision Transformer模型，在大量合成数据集上进行训练，依靠纯数据驱动的学习方式替代显式几何引擎，取代传统的架构级归纳偏置设计。

Result: 该方法同时实现了最先进的立体匹配精度和更优越的运行时效率，证明了纯数据驱动学习能够超越显式工程化的几何方法，打破了归纳偏置在该任务中的必要性假设。

Conclusion: 该工作证明显式归纳偏置不再是立体匹配的必要前提，开创了通过纯数据驱动实现持续优化的新路径，为3D重建领域解锁真正的扩展法则（scaling laws）铺平了道路。

Abstract: Stereo reconstruction is one of the last remaining Computer Vision tasks where all state-of-the-art methods employ a heavy architectural inductive bias. Even though it has been demonstrated that the task can be solved using general-purpose methods, it is widely believed that inductive biases in stereo are strictly necessary for both high-quality results and computational efficiency. We challenge this paradigm.
