In this paper, we demonstrate that both state-of-the-art accuracy and superior runtime efficiency are achievable with a model completely devoid of architectural inductive biases, relying instead on a simple, end-to-end Vision Transformer. By training on massive synthetic datasets, we show that pure data-driven learning can surpass explicitly engineered geometry. This work proves that explicit inductive biases are no longer a prerequisite for stereo matching, ultimately unlocking true scaling laws for continuous improvement in 3D reconstruction.

</details>


### [46] [FractureFields: Contact-Aware Binary Multi-Field Transfer for Fractured 3D Gaussian Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28982)
*Jianchen Wang,Runyang Qu,Fei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FractureFields提出了一种拓扑自适应传输方法，用于处理3D高斯对象断裂后的模拟。通过为每个碎片构建独立的质量和动量场，实现碎片级的P2G/G2P传输，避免跨碎片动量泄漏，并加入动量守恒的接触投影以处理再接触场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Gaussia-MPM管线在断裂后仍然保持单一欧拉速度场，导致断开碎片之间共享插值节点时产生跨碎片动量泄漏，表现为残余粘附和非物理拉伸，无法真实模拟断裂后的动力学行为。

Method: 1) 结构事件后为每个碎片分配持久身份标识；2) 在单次P2G传递中构建碎片特定的质量和动量场；3) 独立推进每个碎片的速度场；4) 进行场感知的G2P更新，确保粒子只采样自己碎片的网格状态；5) 添加动量守恒的接触投影，仅在碎片场相互接近时施加等大反向的法向冲量。

Result: 在重建场景和受控再接触基准测试中，碎片条件化路由从根本上消除了跨碎片混合，接触投影在不引入残余耦合的情况下减少了碰撞期间的相互穿透。

Conclusion: 断裂后的模拟应将结构断开视为局部动力学状态的改变，而不仅仅是本构应力的变化。碎片级独立场传输是更符合物理真实性的模拟范式。

Abstract: Physics-integrated 3D Gaussian representations make it possible to simulate image-reconstructed assets directly as particles, but current Gaussia-MPM pipelines keep a single Eulerian velocity field even after fracture. When disconnected fragments share interpolation support, they still write to and read from the same grid nodes, producing cross-fragment momentum leakage that appears as residual adhesion and non-physical stretching. We present FractureFields, a topology-adaptive transfer for fractured 3D Gaussian objects. After a structural event assigns persistent fragment identities, FractureFields builds fragment-specific mass and momentum fields in a single P2G pass, advances each field independently, and performs a field-aware G2P update so particles only sample their own fragment's grid state. To handle re-contact, we add a momentum-conserving contact projection that applies equal and opposite normal impulses only when two fragment fields are approaching, preserving free separation otherwise. Experiments on reconstructed scenes and a controlled re-contact benchmark show that fragment-conditioned routing eliminates realized cross-fragment mixing by construction, while contact projection reduces interpenetration during collision without reintroducing residual coupling. Overall, we argue that post-fracture simulation should treat structural disconnection as a change in local dynamical state, not merely a change in constitutive stress.

</details>


### [47] [RoSe-SLAM: Robust Semantic-Aware Gaussian Splatting SLAM from Dynamic Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29003)
*Wenting Wang,Jiaxin Guo,Wenzhen Dong,Yun-Hui Liu,Charlie C. L. Wang,Yeung Yam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RoSe-SLAM，一种鲁棒的语义感知高斯溅射SLAM系统，利用2D基础模型的语义特征，通过空间-时间运动掩码生成、遮挡感知关键帧选择和多视图语义一致性模块，实现动态环境下准确的相机追踪和高保真静态场景重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SLAM系统基于静态环境假设，在动态非结构化环境中精度严重下降，现有动态SLAM方法多依赖手工设计的语义标签，难以实现鲁棒的动态物体与静态背景的分离。

Method: 1) 利用2D基础模型蒸馏丰富语义特征到高斯场中，增强动态追踪与建图能力；2) 提出空间-时间运动掩码生成模块，同时捕捉长期运动监控和短期瞬态动态；3) 提出遮挡感知关键帧选择机制和多元语义一致性模块，提升动态环境下建图质量。

Result: 在动态TUM、Bonn和Wild-Mocap基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在轨迹估计和静态场景建图方面均优于现有动态RGB SLAM基线，尤其在长期动态室内环境中表现突出。

Conclusion: 通过结合几何运动线索与语义先验，RoSe-SLAM能够动态过滤不可靠观测并重建精确的静态场景几何，实现动态环境中语义感知的鲁棒SLAM。

Abstract: In dynamic and unstructured environments, conventional SLAM systems generally suffer from significant accuracy degeneration due to their static assumptions. In this work, we propose Robust Semantic-aware Gaussian Splatting SLAM (RoSe-SLAM), to address the dynamic challenge by a holistic semantic scene understanding from uncalibrated monocular inputs, achieving accurate camera tracking and high-quality geometry reconstruction. Unlike conventional semantic SLAM using handcrafted semantic labels, our RoSe-SLAM exploits the semantic feature from 2D foundation model to enhance the dynamic tracking and mapping performance. By distilling the rich semantic features to our Gaussian fields, our method effectively identifies dynamic distractors and achieves semantic-aware multi-view consistency, significantly enhancing the geometric reconstruction and scene inpainting. Specifically, we propose a spatial-temporal motion mask generation module, enabling both long-term motion monitoring and short-term transient dynamics capturing, achieving robust and effective disentanglement of dynamic objects and static backgrounds. During global bundle adjustment, we propose an occlusion-aware keyframe selection mechanism to prioritize the occlusion as metric to pick the keyframes, and a multi-view semantic consistency module to improve the mapping quality in dynamic environments. By combining geometric motion cues with semantic priors, our system dynamically filters unreliable observations and reconstructs accurate static scene geometry. Extensive experiments conducted on benchmark datasets including dynamic TUM, Bonn and Wild-Mocap datasets, demonstrate that our method achieves superior performance in both trajectory estimation and static scene mapping, outperforming existing dynamic RGB SLAM baselines in long-term dynamic indoor environments.

</details>


### [48] [Towards Fully Automated Medical Imaging Code Generation via Validation-based Context Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29016)
*Zixiao Zhao,Jing Sun,Zhe Hou,Cheng-Hao Cai,Qian Liu,Mengze Li,Zijian Zhang,Jin Song Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoMedImg，一个用于医学图像处理代码自动生成的多智能体框架，通过两阶段（规划阶段和编码阶段）多级验证机制实现零人工干预的医疗图像处理流水线生成，在六个医学影像数据集上达到最高0.90的Dice分数和99%的分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在常规程序生成方面表现良好，但在医学图像处理等复杂领域任务中缺乏领域知识和验证机制，需要大量人工干预，导致生成的处理流水线可靠性不足。

Method: AutoMedImg采用多智能体框架，分为两个阶段：规划阶段执行数据集分析和架构设计，包含语义和形式化验证；编码阶段并行生成模块，包含静态检查、执行测试和组装验证。同时引入自动上下文工程（结合领域知识库、共享记忆和验证反馈）以及跨项目自适应流水线合成机制。

Result: 在六个医学影像数据集上使用五种骨干LLM进行广泛评估，AutoMedImg实现了零人工干预，分割任务Dice分数最高达0.90，分类任务准确率达99%。

Conclusion: AutoMedImg通过多级验证和自动上下文工程有效解决了通用LLM在医学图像处理领域的局限性，实现了全自动化的高质量医疗图像处理代码生成。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated considerable promise in program generation for small-scale and conventional application development; however, they remain limited when applied to complex, domain-specific tasks such as medical image processing. General-purpose models lack explicit domain knowledge and robust validation mechanisms to ensure correctness, often requiring substantial human intervention to produce reliable processing pipelines. To address these limitations, we propose AutoMedImg, a multi-agent framework for fully automated medical image processing code generation. AutoMedImg orchestrates specialised agents across two phases: a Planning Phase that performs dataset analysis and architecture design with semantic and formal verification, and a Coding Phase that generates modules in parallel with static checking, execution testing, and assembly validation. This multi-stage validation mitigates error propagation throughout generation, while comprehensive auto-context engineering combining domain-specific knowledge bases, shared memory, and validation feedback automates context construction without manual prompting. A cross-project adaptive pipeline synthesis mechanism further accumulates validated pipelines and retrieves proven components for new tasks based on project similarity, enhancing generation efficiency through cross-project learning. Extensive evaluation across six diverse and well-established medical imaging datasets with five backbone LLMs demonstrates that AutoMedImg achieves zero human intervention, with Dice scores of up to 0.90 for segmentation tasks and 99% accuracy for classification.

</details>


### [49] [Explainable Multi-Loss Distillation Framework for Efficient and Interpretable Shrimp Disease Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29027)
*Anh Nguyen Quynh,Khang Nguyen Quoc,Luyl-Da Quach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SALT框架，利用知识蒸馏的多损失函数进行虾病文本分类，并结合LIME和SHAP可解释性分析，实现早期疾病检测，在性能与效率间取得良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虾病对出口国（尤其是越南）经济影响重大；现有方法多为图像分类，只能在疾病晚期阶段检测，迫切需要基于文本的早期疾病检测方法。

Method: 提出SALT（虾病文本分析+多损失蒸馏）框架，采用知识蒸馏的多损失目标进行文本分类，并集成LIME和SHAP两种可解释性分析方法来评估模型预测和解释学习到的语言特征。

Result: SALT在多个蒸馏目标下取得有竞争力的性能，优于有监督基线，在预测性能与计算效率之间提供了良好权衡；同时展现出强可解释性，能准确识别与疾病描述相关的关键语言特征和语义模式。

Conclusion: 基于知识蒸馏的文本分类在早期虾病诊断及相关研究方向具有广阔应用潜力。

Abstract: Shrimp disease classification has become an urgent issue due to its significant impact on the import-export output of producing countries, particularly Vietnam. Most existing studies focus on image-based classification, which typically operates at the late stage of disease manifestation. Therefore, text-based classification has the potential to enable early and timely disease detection. To address this limitation, we introduce the SALT (Shrimp disease text Analysis with multi-Loss disTillation) framework, which incorporates explainability analysis using Local Interpretable Model-agnostic Explanations (LIME) and SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) to evaluate model predictions and interpret the learned linguistic features. Experimental results demonstrate that SALT achieves competitive performance across multiple distillation objectives, outperforming supervised baselines while providing a favorable trade-off between predictive performance and computational efficiency. Moreover, it exhibits strong explainability, accurately identifying key linguistic features and semantic patterns relevant to disease descriptions. These findings highlight the potential of knowledge distillation-based text classification for future applications in early shrimp disease diagnosis and related research directions.

</details>


### [50] [DocIntent: Answerability-Guided Agentic Restoration for Real-World Document Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29037)
*Zihan Huang,Shihang Wu,Junle Liu,Peirong Zhang,Yongxin Shi,Xuhan Zheng,Lianwen Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DocIntent，一个无需训练的、基于可回答性引导的智能体恢复框架，用于改善多模态大语言模型在真实世界降质文档上的视觉问答能力。通过评估问题可回答性、识别任务相关降质并选择性调用恢复工具，配合基于比较的回滚机制，该框架无需额外的降质分类器或图像质量评估模型，即可稳定提升多种开源和闭源MLLM在WildDoc基准上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界中的文档降质（模糊、阴影、畸变、摩尔纹等）严重损害MLLM的文档问答能力。虽然直觉上可以先恢复再问答，但现有恢复方法需要人工设计策略且依赖领域知识；已有的智能体恢复框架主要面向自然图像并追求感知质量，忽视了恢复应服务于下游任务而非优化通用图像质量指标。

Method: DocIntent框架包含三个核心模块：(1) 可回答性评估——先判断问题在当前文档状态下是否可回答；(2) 任务相关降质识别与选择性工具调用——识别影响回答的降质类型并针对性调用恢复工具；(3) 基于比较的回滚机制——验证每个恢复步骤的效果，若问题相关证据变得难以辨认则回退该步骤。整个过程无需额外的预训练降质分类器或图像质量评估模型，是一种训练免费的框架。

Result: 在WildDoc基准上的大量实验表明，DocIntent能够一致地提升多种开源和闭源MLLM的平均得分和一致性。

Conclusion: 智能体恢复应面向下游任务导向而非通用图像质量优化，DocIntent通过可回答性引导和比较回滚机制，为真实世界降质文档的VQA提供了一种无需训练、无需额外模型的高效解决方案，验证了任务感知的智能体恢复在文档问答中的价值。

Abstract: Real-world degradations such as blur, shadow, distortion, and moire patterns severely impair the document question-answering capabilities of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). Applying restoration tools before Visual Question Answering (VQA) is an intuitive solution. However, existing restoration approaches remain limited, as manually designing and executing restoration strategies is labor-intensive and requires domain expertise. Agentic restoration offers new possibilities for automation, yet existing frameworks primarily target natural images and pursue perceptual quality, overlooking that restoration should serve downstream tasks rather than optimize generic image quality metrics. To this end, we explore the value of agentic restoration for real-world degraded document VQA and propose DocIntent, a training-free Answerability-Guided Agentic Restoration framework. DocIntent first assesses question answerability, then identifies task-relevant degradations and selectively invokes restoration tools. A Comparison-Based Rollback mechanism validates each restoration step and reverts it when question-relevant evidence becomes less decipherable. The entire process requires no additional pretrained degradation classifier or image quality assessment model. Extensive experiments on the WildDoc benchmark show that DocIntent consistently improves the average score and consistency of different open- and closed-source MLLMs. The code and experimental data will be publicly available.

</details>


### [51] [sRGB Real Noise Modeling via Noise-Aware Sampling with Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29038)
*Dongjin Kim,Donggoo Jung,Sungyong Baik,Tae Hyun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于归一化流（NF）的新型框架，通过估计底层相机设置来改进真实图像噪声建模，既能对噪声进行分类也能生成多样化噪声图像，在去噪基准数据集上取得了领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CNN去噪方法假设噪声遵循已知分布，难以处理真实世界噪声；虽然已有生成方法（GAN、NF）用于生成真实噪声图像，但现有方法依赖相机元数据，即使在采样阶段也需要元数据支持，限制了实用性。

Method: 提出一种新的归一化流（NF）框架，能够同时估计底层相机设置，从而改进噪声建模，并基于估计的相机设置生成多种多样的噪声分布。该框架支持两个功能：基于相机设置对噪声进行分类，以及生成各种噪声图像。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的模型在噪声质量方面表现优异，并在基准数据集上的去噪性能中处于领先地位。

Conclusion: 通过估计相机设置而非依赖元数据，该NF框架克服了现有方法在采样阶段对元数据的依赖问题，实现了更好的真实噪声建模和更优的去噪性能。

Abstract: Noise poses a widespread challenge in signal processing, particularly when it comes to denoising images. Although convolutional neural networks (CNNs) have exhibited remarkable success in this field, they are predicated upon the belief that noise follows established distributions, which restricts their practicality when dealing with real-world noise. To overcome this limitation, several efforts have been taken to collect noisy image datasets from the real world. Generative methods, employing techniques such as generative adversarial networks (GANs) and normalizing flows (NFs), have emerged as a solution for generating realistic noisy images. Recent works model noise using camera metadata, however requiring metadata even for sampling phase. In contrast, in this work, we aim to estimate the underlying camera settings, enabling us to improve noise modeling and generate diverse noise distributions. To this end, we introduce a new NF framework that allows us to both classify noise based on camera settings and generate various noisy images. Through experimental results, our model demonstrates exceptional noise quality and leads in denoising performance on benchmark datasets.

</details>


### [52] [Di$^2$CycleSB: Towards High-Quality Unsupervised Nighttime Visibility Enhancement via Schrödinger Bridge Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29043)
*Hanting Li,Xin Sun,Wei Ye,Jungong Han,Liang-jie Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Di²CycleSB，一种基于无监督Cycle Schrödinger Bridge Transformer框架的夜间可见度增强方法，利用动态积分图像先验估计光源效应，无需正则化约束和图像分解即可实现端到端的光效应抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 夜间可见度增强中，光效应污染是重大挑战。现有方法通过先验驱动正则化估计和分解光效应，但受限于手工设计的先验和分解问题的病态性，难以取得理想效果。

Method: 1) 提出新型光效应估计器，通过聚合动态积分图像表示来参数化高斯型自适应先验，用于非均匀辉光估计；2) 提出先验信息生成器，利用光效应表示引导Transformer块内的长距离依赖建模；3) 将光效应抑制形式化为Schrödinger桥问题，构建带有循环一致性约束的正反向桥以实现增强。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的大量实验证明了Di²CycleSB在夜间可见度增强方面的显著有效性，实现了无需任何正则化约束和图像分解的端到端光效应抑制。

Conclusion: Di²CycleSB通过无监督循环Schrödinger桥框架结合动态积分图像先验，成功克服了传统方法依赖手工先验和病态分解的局限，为夜间可见度增强提供了高质量的无监督解决方案。

Abstract: Light-effect contamination poses a significant challenge to nighttime visibility enhancement. Most methods suppress light effects by estimating and decomposing them through prior-driven regularization, yet they are often limited by hand-crafted priors and ill-posed nature of decomposition. This work proposes Di$^2$CycleSB, a unsupervised Cycle Schrödinger Bridge Transformer framework guided by dynamic integral image priors, for high-quality unsupervised nighttime visibility enhancement. Specifically, a novel light-effect estimator is introduced to parameterize Gaussian-like adaptive priors by aggregating dynamic integral image representations for non-uniform glow estimation. Then, we propose a prior-informed Generator that exploits light-effect representations to guide long-range dependency modeling within our specific Transformer blocks. We formulate light-effect suppression as a Schrödinger bridge problem and construct forward and backward bridges with cycle consistency constraints to achieve visually pleasing enhancement. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate the remarkable effectiveness of our Di$^2$CycleSB in enhancing nighttime visibility. In particular, it achieves effective end-to-end light-effect suppression without any regularization constraints and image decomposition. The code and models are available at https://github.com/LHTcode/Di2CycleSB.

</details>


### [53] [DReSG: Diffusion Residuals for Stylized Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29048)
*Zhongliang Liu,Wenjie Liu,Yang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了DReSG框架，通过3D-ground的残差反馈机制实现参考引导的3D高斯泼溅风格化，兼顾风格表达、场景结构保持和跨视角稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VGG特征方法虽然稳定但缺乏丰富风格表达，扩散模型虽然具有强大先验但直接用于3D风格化会导致视角漂移、局部伪影和外观更新难控制的问题。

Method: DReSG将注意力引导的扩散提案表示为相对于当前渲染的残差目标，并通过多视角高斯反馈逐步将残差吸收到共享高斯场景中；在目标构建时调节残差强度，在多视角拟合时结合覆盖感知的视角选择与冲突过滤的颜色更新。

Result: 实验表明DReSG在参考引导风格化上达到有竞争力的效果，同时更好地保持了场景结构和跨视角稳定性。

Conclusion: DReSG通过3D-ground的残差反馈机制有效结合了扩散模型的强先验与高斯泼溅的可控性，解决了以往方法的视角漂移和伪影问题。

Abstract: Reference-guided stylization of scenes represented by 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is important for efficient and controllable 3D content creation. Existing VGG-feature-based 3D stylization methods provide stable rendered-view optimization, but often under-represent expressive reference style cues; diffusion models offer stronger image priors, yet direct per-view or score-based diffusion guidance can lead to view drift, local artifacts, and hard-to-control appearance updates. We present DReSG, a 3D-grounded residual-feedback framework for stylized Gaussian splatting. DReSG represents attention-guided diffusion proposals as residual targets relative to the current render, and progressively absorbs these residuals into a shared Gaussian scene through multi-view Gaussian feedback. To make this feedback stable and controllable, DReSG modulates residual strength during target construction and combines coverage-aware view selection with conflict-filtered color updates during multi-view fitting. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DReSG achieves competitive reference-guided stylization while better preserving scene structure and cross-view stability. Our project page is available at https://vpx-ecnu.github.io/DReSG-website/.

</details>


### [54] [AdapToPASS: Ambiguity-aware Adaptive Spherical Transformer for Panoramic Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29081)
*Soumyaratna Debnath,Weiming Zhang,Shriram Damodaran,Dingwen Xiao,Addison Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AdapToPASS，一种受生物感知启发的球形Transformer框架，用于全景语义分割。它通过自适应球形注意力（AdaSpA）动态调节注意力以应对上下文歧义，采用双焦点球形表示和边界监督解决几何歧义，在未见球形变换下较次优方法分别提升+13.38%和+18.77%的相对mIoU，并推出参数量小于2M的轻量版本AdapToPASS-Swift。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有球形Transformer假设规范球形结构和稳定视点，但在真实图像中相机运动不受约束，导致上下文和几何歧义，缺乏自适应机制以应对此类歧义，鲁棒性受限。受生物感知的对歧义感知的特性启发，作者希望构建能够自适应建模上下文与几何歧义的模型。

Method: 首先系统分析现有PASS架构在各种未见球形变换下的表现；然后提出AdapToPASS框架。核心包括：1）自适应球形注意力（AdaSpA）块，根据局部上下文歧义动态调制注意力；2）双焦点球形表示（Bifocal Spherical Representation），在视场与空间分辨率间取得平衡；3）借鉴生物视觉的边界敏感性，引入边界监督。另提出轻量变体AdapToPASS-Swift。

Result: 在室内和室外语义分割任务上均一致超越先前最先进方法。在未见球形变换下，AdapToPASS在Stanford2D3D上超越次优方法+13.38%相对mIoU，在WildPASS上+18.77%。AdapToPASS-Swift参数量少于2M，在超越紧凑基线的同时保持对球形变换的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: AdapToPASS验证了将生物感知的歧义自适应机制引入球形Transformer的有效性，显著提升了全景语义分割在复杂球形变换下的鲁棒性与准确性，同时轻量版本证明了高效部署的可行性。

Abstract: Spherical Transformers have emerged as a promising framework for panoramic semantic segmentation (PASS) by operating directly on spherical geometry and alleviating projection-induced distortions. However, existing architectures often assume canonical spherical structure and stable viewpoints, which are frequently violated in real-world imagery due to unconstrained camera motion, introducing contextual and geometric ambiguity. Consequently, they lack adaptive mechanisms to handle such ambiguity, limiting robustness to unseen spherical transformations. In contrast, biological perception is inherently ambiguity-aware, adapting to fluctuations in cue reliability caused by geometric and contextual variations to maintain stable interpretation under complex transformations. Motivated by this, we first systematically analyze existing PASS architectures under various unseen spherical transformations. We then introduce AdapToPASS, a novel bio-inspired Spherical Transformer that adaptively models contextual and geometric ambiguities for robust PASS. At its core, Adaptive Spherical Attention (AdaSpA) blocks dynamically modulate attention according to local contextual ambiguity, mimicking adaptive, context-driven biological perception. To address geometric ambiguity, AdapToPASS employs Bifocal Spherical Representation to balance field of view and spatial resolution, together with boundary supervision inspired by the boundary-sensitive nature of biological vision. Across indoor and outdoor semantic segmentation, AdapToPASS consistently outperforms prior state-of-the-art methods. Under unseen spherical transformations, it surpasses the next-best method by +13.38% relative mIoU on Stanford2D3D and +18.77% on WildPASS. We further introduce AdapToPASS-Swift, a lightweight variant with fewer than 2M parameters, which surpasses compact baselines while retaining robustness to spherical transformations.

</details>


### [55] [Elastic Triangle Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29106)
*Tian Shi,Shenhan Qian,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了三角形光栅化（Triangle Splatting）中的核函数设计，提出了具有双边梯度支持和自适应边界值的弹性核函数（Elastic Kernel），在网格优化和三角形汤优化两个任务中均优于现有核函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的三角形光栅化方法在核函数选择上不一致，且对核函数优化行为的分析仅限于无结构的三角形汤在新视图合成上的应用，缺乏系统性的核函数设计对比与分析。

Method: 将三角形光栅化视为通用的光度优化工具，通过两个互补任务（网格优化的形状重建和三角形汤优化的新视图合成）对比不同核函数的性能；同时提出一种弹性核函数，具有跨边界的双边梯度支持和自适应边界值。

Result: 在隔离对比下，弹性核函数在形状重建任务中全面优于现有核函数，并在大多数新视图合成基准上取得最佳表现，验证了核函数设计对三角形光栅化有效性和通用性的重要性。

Conclusion: 核函数设计是三角形光栅化性能的关键因素，弹性核函数凭借其双边梯度支持和自适应边界值，能够更稳健地优化网格表示，扩展了三角形光栅化在通用光度优化任务中的适用性。

Abstract: While neural rendering methods such as 3D Gaussian Splatting achieve remarkable visual fidelity, traditional polygonal meshes remain the backbone of established graphics pipelines. Triangle splatting bridges this gap by optimizing triangle primitives as differentiable splats, producing representations that are closer to mesh-based workflows. Central to these methods is the kernel function that softens triangle boundaries to propagate gradients to vertex positions. Existing triangle splatting methods make inconsistent choices of kernel functions, and analysis of these kernels' optimization behavior has been limited to unstructured triangle soups for novel-view synthesis. In this work, we consider triangle splatting as a generic tool for photometric optimization, comparing kernel properties through two complementary tasks: mesh optimization for shape reconstruction and triangle soup optimization for novel-view synthesis. Along with the analysis, we introduce an elastic kernel function that features bilateral gradient support across the boundary and an adaptive boundary value, which are shown to be essential for robust optimization. Under isolated comparison, our elastic kernel outperforms existing kernels on shape reconstruction and in the majority of novel-view synthesis benchmarks, demonstrating the importance of kernel design in the effectiveness and versatility of triangle splatting.

</details>


### [56] [NFAD: Nuisance-Filtered Anomaly Detection Under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29112)
*Dat Cao,Son Nghiem,Phan Nguyen,Jun Rekimoto,Jhih-Ciang Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出NFAD框架，通过显式建模并抑制成像条件变化引起的干扰子空间，解决分布偏移下的工业异常检测问题，在AeBAD-S基准上达到91.0%的SOTA图像级AUROC，同时不牺牲标准基准上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业异常检测基准在标准条件下已趋于饱和，但实验室环境与真实部署场景存在差距。光照、背景、视角等环境因素的变化会使正常样本偏离训练分布，导致伪异常响应，而现有方法缺乏针对此类分布偏移的有效处理。

Method: NFAD框架在特征空间中显式建模干扰变化。通过内容保持的扰动生成匹配的特征位移，估计干扰子空间，并在推理时抑制其对异常残差的贡献。框架包含两个互补分支：全投影用于图像级检测，选择性抑制用于像素级定位，从而保留局部缺陷的证据。

Result: 在AeBAD-S基准上，NFAD达到91.0%的图像级AUROC，刷新SOTA记录。在不需要显式评估分布偏移的标准基准（VisA、Real-IAD、MVTec AD）上，NFAD仍保持具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 显式抑制干扰变化可以显著提升分布偏移下的异常检测鲁棒性，同时不牺牲标准场景下的检测性能，证明了该方法的实际部署价值。

Abstract: Recent advances in anomaly detection (AD) for industrial inspection have pushed performance on standard benchmarks toward saturation. However, strong benchmark performance does not necessarily translate to real-world deployment, as these benchmarks are primarily collected under controlled acquisition conditions. Changes in illumination, background, viewpoint, and other environmental factors can shift normal samples away from the learned normal distribution and cause false anomaly responses. We address AD under such distribution shifts by explicitly modeling nuisance variation from changing imaging conditions in feature space. Without anomaly labels or target-domain data, our Nuisance-Filtered Anomaly Detection (NFAD) framework estimates a nuisance subspace from matched feature displacements induced by content-preserving perturbations and suppresses its contribution to anomaly residuals at inference. The same subspace supports two complementary branches: full projection for image-level detection and selective suppression for pixel-level localization, preserving evidence of localized defects. On AeBAD-S, a benchmark specifically designed for AD under acquisition shifts, NFAD achieves 91.0\% image-level AUROC, establishing a new state of the art. Notably, this robustness does not come at the expense of conventional AD performance: NFAD remains competitive on standard benchmarks that do not explicitly evaluate distribution shift, including VisA, Real-IAD, and MVTec AD. These results show that explicitly suppressing such nuisance variation improves AD under distribution shift while preserving strong performance in standard settings.

</details>


### [57] [GramLoop: Training-Free Gram-Gated Replay for Robust Dense Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29113)
*Yang Chen,Canyu Shen,Xinzhe Rao,Yuanyi Yan,Yunlu Chen,Meng Tang,Teng Long,Vincent Tao Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GramLoop，一种无需训练的推理时优化框架，通过在冻结的DINOv3骨干网络内重复执行短Transformer窗口并利用最终层余弦-Gram一致性来控制每次重放，从而在不改变模型权重、任务适配器或预测头的情况下，提升密集预测模型在分布偏移下的鲁棒性。在5个分布偏移基准上均优于基线，在COCO-O上mAP提升+0.252。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有密集预测模型（如DINOv3）在分布偏移下性能下降，而常用的适应方法需要修改权重或任务适配器。作者希望在推理阶段直接在视觉骨干内部增加计算量来提升鲁棒性，同时不改变任何模型组件，且需避免重复Transformer计算破坏DINOv3用于保持空间结构的成对patch关系。

Method: GramLoop框架采用"重放-门控"机制：在冻结的DINOv3骨干中回放一个短Transformer窗口，每个候选输出经由冻结后缀传播后，与标准DINOv3轨迹进行最终层余弦-Gram一致性度量，最后在重放窗口端点通过patchwise门控决定是否接受该候选。整个过程无需训练。

Result: 在目标检测和语义分割任务上，涵盖退化（corruptions）、扰动（perturbations）和自然分布偏移（natural shifts）共5个基准，GramLoop全部优于对应的DINOv3基线。COCO-O上mAP提升+0.252，Effective Robustness提升+0.250，同时保持干净的ADE20K性能不下降。

Conclusion: 通过推理时计算增加而非权重调整，GramLoop能够在不牺牲干净域性能的前提下显著提升密集预测模型的分布偏移鲁棒性，验证了训练免费、权重冻结条件下利用骨干内部计算增强鲁棒性的可行性。

Abstract: We aim to improve frozen DINOv3 dense-prediction models under distribution shift by adding inference computation inside the visual backbone, without changing model weights, task adapters, or prediction heads. The challenge is that repeated transformer-block computation must refine dense features without disrupting the pairwise patch relations that DINOv3 uses to preserve spatial structure. We introduce GramLoop, a training-free framework that replays a short transformer window and controls each replay through final-layer cosine-Gram consistency. Each proposal is propagated through the frozen suffix, measured against the standard DINOv3 trajectory, and accepted through a patchwise gate at the replay-window endpoint. Across object detection and semantic segmentation under corruptions, perturbations, and natural shifts, GramLoop improves all five shifted benchmarks over the paired DINOv3 baseline. On COCO-O, it improves mAP by +0.252 and Effective Robustness by +0.250, while preserving clean ADE20K performance. Code will be released.

</details>


### [58] [Acoustically Grounded Cost Learning for Open-Vocabulary Audio-Visual Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29121)
*Tianrui Hui,Shaofei Huang,Qisong Han,Yaxiong Wang,Lechao Cheng,Zhedong Zheng,Zhun Zhong,Richang Hong,Meng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的开放词汇视听语义分割（OV-AVSS）方法AGCL，通过将目标从类别无关的前景定义重构为类别特定的音响接地成本学习，解决以往方法中语义混杂正样本导致的不稳定问题，在AVSBench-OV数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往方法依赖类别无关的前景定义，将语义多样化的对象归入异质正样本集合，导致模型学习到不稳定的发声模式并产生不可靠的候选区域。因此需要将目标重新定义为类别特定，以更准确地分割开放式类别中的发声物体。

Method: 提出Acoustically Grounded Cost Learning（AGCL）框架：1）通过Audio-Modulated Cost Generation（AMCG）和Audio-Guided Temporal Aggregation（AGTA）模块实现类别内发声性发现，进行帧级发声区域高亮和视频级时间细化，采用低侵入式音频注入机制；2）引入Synergistic Distractor Mining（SDM）策略进行类别间干扰物区分，选择性惩罚声学上和语义上易混淆的负类别，学习更具判别性的决策边界。

Result: 在AVSBench-OV数据集上的大量实验表明，AGCL方法显著优于先前的最先进方法，尤其在未见类别上表现突出。

Conclusion: 将OV-AVSS问题重新表述为类别特定目标并采用音频接地的成本学习框架，能够有效克服异质正样本问题，提升开放词汇场景下视听语义分割的准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Open-Vocabulary Audio-Visual Semantic Segmentation (OV-AVSS) aims to perform pixel-level segmentation of sound-emitting objects from an open set of categories. The previous method relies on a class-agnostic foreground definition, which groups semantically diverse objects into a heterogeneous positive set, causing the model to learn unstable sounding patterns and produce unreliable proposals. To address this, we reformulate the objective to be category-specific and propose a novel Acoustically Grounded Cost Learning (AGCL) framework to transform the static, audio-agnostic visual-text priors into dynamic, audio-grounded cost representations. For intra-category soundingness discovery, we devise Audio-Modulated Cost Generation (AMCG) and Audio-Guided Temporal Aggregation (AGTA) modules to enable both frame-level sounding region highlighting and video-level temporal refinement with a low-intrusive audio injection mechanism. For inter-category distractor discrimination, we introduce a Synergistic Distractor Mining (SDM) strategy, which selectively penalizes acoustically and semantically confusing negative categories to learn more discriminative decision boundaries. Extensive experiments on the AVSBench-OV dataset demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms previous state-of-the-art approaches, particularly on unseen categories. Code is available at https://github.com/spyflying/AGCL.

</details>


### [59] [Dancing Stick Figures: An Introductory Dataset for Training Video Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29123)
*Jin Hyuk Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Dancing Stick Figures，一个合成视频数据集，旨在解决视频生成模型训练中三大障碍：训练迭代周期长、数据难以获取、评分指标粗糙。数据集包含4020个64x64、64帧的视频片段及其丰富的逐帧注释（关节位置、深度、法线、部件标签），支持可解释的生成质量评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型从零训练面临三个固有障碍：1) 反馈回路长，一次训练失败需要重新运行整个训练流程；2) 数据难以获取，大规模真实语料往往未公开；3) 评分方式粗糙，开放式生成缺乏单一正确输出，聚合分数无法定位具体失败原因。需要一个针对性的合成数据集来逐一攻破这些障碍。

Method: 构建一个合成视频数据集Dancing Stick Figures：1) 选择64x64、64帧的小规模参考任务，使训练可在单工作站GPU上反复进行以加速迭代；2) 发布0.79GB的训练数据（4020个视频片段，1340个六秒源动作，每个动作由三个相机通过确定性数据生成管线和渲染）；3) 提供检查点和Colab工作流，可在16GB Tesla T4上以缩减预算重跑参考训练流程；4) 每个帧保留完整生成状态（ARDY cskel27关节位置、相机参数、身体参数、源动作），并附带逐像素深度、表面法线和部件标签。

Result: 数据集注释支持设计针对可见拓扑和部件级运动的专用评估指标；通过引入损坏（corruptions）来测试这些指标的敏感性，揭示了其盲点和局限性。

Conclusion: Dancing Stick Figures通过合成数据策略，在数据规模、迭代速度、可解释评估三个维度上为视频生成模型研究提供了可控、可复现的基准方案。

Abstract: Training a video-generation model from scratch is hard for reasons that precede model design. The feedback loop is long: a failure that appears only after a training run can make each attempted fix another run. The data are hard to reach: the corpora and recipes behind strong models are large, heterogeneous, and often unreleased. And scoring is blunt: open-ended generation has no single correct output, and an aggregate score does not by itself establish whether a sample succeeds or which property failed. Dancing Stick Figures is a synthetic video dataset built against these three obstacles. For iteration speed, its 64x64, 64-frame reference task is sized for practical repeated training on a single workstation GPU. For accessibility, the release is a 0.79-GB training tier of 4,020 video clips--1,340 six-second source motions, each rendered from three cameras by a deterministic dataset-generation harness--with checkpoints and a Colab workflow that reruns the reference training pipeline at reduced budget on a 16 GB Tesla T4. For scoring, every frame retains its generating state (ARDY cskel27 joint positions, camera, body parameters, and source motion) and per-pixel depth, surface normals, and part labels. These annotations support dataset-specific metrics for visible topology and part-wise motion; corruptions expose their sensitivities and blind spots.

</details>


### [60] [Efficient Language-to-Vision Feature Injection for Referring Single-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29126)
*Han Wang,Yuxuan Liu,Yuhan Sun,Jian Yang,Xiaotong Xu,Yixuan Lv,Zhuang Zhou,Shengyang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LVTrack，一个纯Transformer框架，通过模式条件门控特征注入器自适应调节文本引导以缓解语义漂移，直接利用冻结的视觉-语言预训练模型降低训练成本，并集成混合相对-绝对位置编码和轻量级记忆机制改进时间定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指代单目标跟踪的核心难点在于语言在不同阶段的作用不同：语言对于目标接地至关重要，但在跟踪过程中过度强调会引发语义漂移。同时现有方法往往需要昂贵的视觉-语言对齐训练。

Method: 1) 提出模式条件门控特征注入器（Gated Feature Injector）自适应调节文本引导以缓解语义漂移；2) 直接利用冻结的视觉-语言预训练模型，配以针对性适配，降低训练成本并保留语言理解能力；3) 集成混合相对-绝对位置编码与轻量级记忆机制改善时间定位；4) 使用高斯平滑KL损失优化自回归框预测。

Result: 在标准基准上的大量实验表明LVTrack取得了强性能。

Conclusion: LVTrack通过纯Transformer框架和冻结预训练模型的有效利用，在降低训练成本的同时实现了指代单目标跟踪的强性能，验证了自适应门控机制和轻量级记忆设计在缓解语义漂移和改善时间定位方面的有效性。

Abstract: Referring single-object tracking enables language-grounded target initialization and subsequent tracking by jointly leveraging semantic cues and visual templates. The core difficulty is to use language differently across stages: it is indispensable for grounding but can induce semantic drift during tracking when overemphasized. Meanwhile, current methods often require costly vision-language alignment training. We present LVTrack, a pure transformer framework that introduces a mode-conditioned Gated Feature Injector to adaptively regulate textual guidance and alleviate semantic drift. Together with targeted adaptations, it directly harnesses a frozen vision-language pretrained model, greatly reducing training cost and preserving strong language understanding. To further improve temporal localization, LVTrack integrates hybrid relative-absolute positional encodings with a lightweight memory mechanism and optimizes autoregressive box prediction using a Gaussian-smoothed KL loss. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that LVTrack achieves strong performance.

</details>


### [61] [Chat-Edit-3D++: Interactive 3D and 4D Scene Editing via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29137)
*Shuangkang Fang,Yufeng Wang,Yi-Hsuan Tsai,Wenrui Ding,Yi Yang,Shuchang Zhou,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CE3D++，一种基于大语言模型的对话式3D场景编辑方法。通过Hash-Atlas网络将3D场景编辑重构为2D贴图操作，解耦2D编辑与3D重建流程，使LLM能够根据用户任意文本输入自主调用多达30种视觉工具。该方法还扩展到单目4D场景，通过轨迹数据集微调LLM实现运动约束，实验证明其具备强场景理解与多轮对话能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景编辑方案存在以下不足：1）输入模式固定，文本输入灵活性受限；2）编辑能力受限于单一或少数2D视觉模型；3）需要复杂的流水线设计将视觉模型集成到3D重建过程。这些问题阻碍了3D场景编辑作为交互式设计工具的进一步发展。

Method: 1）提出Hash-Atlas网络，将3D场景编辑转化为2D贴图图像上的操作，实现2D编辑与3D重建流程的解耦；2）提出对话式方法CE3D++，以LLM为核心，理解用户任意文本输入并自主调用对应视觉模型；3）扩展到单目4D场景，对移动物体施加运动约束；4）创建编辑相关轨迹数据集微调LLM，使较小规模的LLM能准确调度多达30种视觉工具。

Result: 实验结果表明CE3D++能有效集成多个视觉模型实现多样化的视觉编辑效果，具备强大的场景理解能力和多轮对话能力，并成功支持多达30种视觉工具的准确调度。

Conclusion: CE3D++通过解耦2D编辑与3D重建流程，并以LLM为中心实现灵活的对话式3D场景编辑，有效解决了现有方法输入模式固定、模型集成复杂等问题，同时扩展到4D场景，为3D场景编辑作为交互式设计工具提供了新途径。代码与模型已开源。

Abstract: Recent work on image content manipulation based on vision-language pre-training models has been effectively extended to text-driven 3D scene editing. However, existing schemes for 3D scene editing still have certain shortcomings, hindering their further development as interactive design tools. Such schemes typically adhere to fixed input patterns, limiting flexibility in text input. Furthermore, their editing capabilities are constrained by a single or a few 2D visual models and require intricate pipeline design to integrate these models into 3D reconstruction processes. To address the aforementioned issues, we propose the Hash-Atlas network, which reformulates 3D scene editing as operations on 2D atlas images, thereby achieving a workflow decoupling of the 2D editing and 3D reconstruction processes. Building on this foundation, we introduce a dialogue-based 3D scene editing approach, termed CE3D++, which is centered on a large language model (LLM) that allows arbitrary textual input from users and interprets their intentions, subsequently facilitating the autonomous invocation of the corresponding visual models. Additionally, we extend CE3D++ to monocular 4D scenes by imposing motion constraints on moving objects and further fine-tuning the LLM by creating a trajectory dataset related to editing tasks, which enables the smaller LLM to schedule up to 30 different visual tools accurately. Experimental results demonstrate that CE3D++ effectively integrates multiple visual models to achieve diverse visual editing effects, possessing strong scene comprehension and multi-round dialog capabilities. The source codes and trained models are available at https://github.com/Fangkang515/CE3D.

</details>


### [62] [STARLINC: Satellite Trail Artifact Removal using Inter-Frame Correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29145)
*Shingeon Kim,Hyeyoon Lee,Dain Kwon,Kanghyun Choi,Sunjong Park,Mi-Ryang Kim,Jeong-Eun Lee,Jinho Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出STARLINC，首个无需像素级标注的机器学习框架，用于自动去除低轨卫星（如Starlink）在巡天图像中留下的轨迹。该方法结合合成卫星轨迹生成、时间相邻曝光的帧间差分图以及热图定位线索，在真实数据上显著优于基线方法，为下一代天文巡天提供可扩展的轨迹去除方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低轨卫星数量激增，严重污染天文巡天图像；传统人工筛查因数据量庞大（每晚TB级）不可行；现有通用线检测方法因天文图像灰度、稀疏亮星和低信噪比等特征存在域不匹配而难以泛化；从头训练新模型又受限于缺乏大规模标注数据集。

Method: STARLINC框架包含三部分：(1) 合成卫星轨迹生成用于训练数据增广，避免依赖人工像素级标注；(2) 利用时间相邻曝光的帧间差分图突出瞬态轨迹；(3) 引入热图提供额外的定位线索，辅助像素级分割。

Result: 在真实世界数据上的大量实验表明，STARLINC相较于基线方法取得了显著提升，验证了其作为可扩展解决方案的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: STARLINC是首个无需密集像素级标注的机器学习卫星轨迹去除框架，有效解决了域不匹配和数据稀缺两大挑战，为下一代天文巡天提供了可扩展的自动化轨迹去除方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of low Earth orbit satellites such as Starlink is increasingly contaminating astronomical surveys. In practice, contaminated images are often identified through inspection. However, modern surveys generate terabytes of data each night, making manual screening infeasible and necessitating reliable automated methods for satellite trail removal. Unfortunately, existing general-domain line detection methods fail to generalize to astronomical images due to domain mismatch, which are mostly grayscale with sparse bright stars and have a low signal-to-noise ratio. Moreover, training new models from scratch is impractical due to the lack of large-scale annotated astronomical datasets. To address these challenges, we introduce STARLINC, the first ML-based framework for satellite trail removal without requiring tedious pixel-level annotation of astronomical images. STARLINC combines synthetic satellite trail generation for training, inter-frame differential maps from temporally adjacent exposures to highlight transient trails, and heatmaps to provide additional localization cues for pixel-level segmentation. Extensive experiments on real-world data demonstrate substantial improvements over baselines, establishing STARLINC as a scalable solution for next-generation astronomical surveys. Code is available at https://github.com/starioKim/STARLINC.

</details>


### [63] [SGRNet: Spatially Guided Radiology Network for Structured Radiological Reporting of Head and Neck Cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29153)
*Ayush Gupta,Vinkle Srivastav,Prateek Upadhya,Amit Gupta,Krithika Rangarajan,Nicolas Padoy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对头颈癌（HNC）的放射学报告自动生成问题，提出将自由文本生成重构为解剖学引导的结构化多标签报告任务，并设计了SGRNet（空间引导放射学网络），通过自动器官分割和3D高斯热图弱监督肿瘤定位图两种低成本空间先验来弥补PET代谢成像缺失的不足。在多中心184例配对CECT数据上，SGRNet在五个临床关键解剖亚区上mAP达到0.60，较强基线提升8.8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化放射报告生成可减轻临床负担并消除观察者间差异，但标准自由文本生成模型在密集区域存在幻觉风险，且数据稀缺条件下表现不佳。头颈癌（HNC）的CECT成像缺乏代谢信息（如PET），进一步加剧了视觉信息的不足。

Method: 将报告生成重构为基于解剖学先验的多标签结构化报告任务：预测层次化临床模式下的局部肿瘤侵犯；提出SGRNet，整合自动器官分割和弱监督肿瘤定位图（3D高斯热图建模）两种低代价空间先验，通过空间特征调制动态融合，引导网络关注肿瘤引起的细微结构改变。

Result: 在184例多中心配对的HNC CECT体积与报告数据集上，针对五个临床意义重大且密集排列的解剖亚区，SGRNet获得0.60的平均精度（mAP），较仅使用体积的3D强基线实现了8.8个百分点的绝对提升。

Conclusion: 空间引导的先验信息能够有效弥补代谢成像缺失带来的视觉空白，解剖学结构化的报告任务设计在保证事实安全性（避免幻觉）和应对数据稀缺方面优于标准自由文本生成方法。

Abstract: Automated radiological report generation can alleviate clinical workloads and eliminate observer variability. However, standard free-text generation models pose hallucination risks in dense regions and fail under data scarcity. We address these challenges in Head and Neck Cancer (HNC) from contrast-enhanced CT (CECT) imaging. To enforce factual safety, we reformulate report generation as an anatomically grounded, multi-label, structured reporting task, predicting localized tumor involvement across a hierarchical clinical schema. To bridge the visual gap from missing metabolic imaging (e.g., PET), we introduce SGRNet (Spatially Guided Radiology Network), incorporating two low-cost spatial priors: automated organ segmentations and weakly supervised tumor localization maps modeled via 3D Gaussian heatmaps. These priors are dynamically integrated via spatial feature modulation to guide the network toward subtle tumor-induced structural alterations. Evaluated on a multi-centric dataset of 184 paired HNC CECT volumes and reports, on five clinically salient, densely packed anatomical subsites, SGRNet achieves a mean Average Precision (mAP) of 0.60, an 8.8 percentage-point absolute improvement over strong volume-only 3D baselines.

</details>


### [64] [Training-Free Hidden-State Refinement for Flow-Matching Image Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29160)
*Yuanyi Yan,Xinzhe Rao,Canyu Shen,Yang Chen,Yunlu Chen,Meng Tang,Teng Long,Vincent Tao Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的循环框架（Token Loop），通过在冻结的flow-matching图像生成模型中重复应用选定的Transformer层，在不改变模型权重和外层采样器的前提下，将额外的推理计算注入去噪器内部，从而提升生成质量，超越了单纯增加采样步数的传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的flow-matching图像生成器通常通过增加采样步数来消耗额外的测试时间计算，但这种方式会反复评估整个去噪器，将质量提升与采样器成本耦合在一起。作者希望在不改变模型权重和采样器的前提下，找到一种利用额外计算来提升生成质量的方法。

Method: 提出一个训练免费的循环框架，在每次去噪调用中重复应用选定的Transformer层。具体包含：Dense和Sparse Token Loop控制token范围；Sampling-Progress Gating和循环层范围决定循环在何时何地生效；循环次数和强度控制重复更新的程度；Loop Guidance将普通与循环的向量场预测结合。

Result: 在两种Scale-RAE模型规模上，循环变体提升了主要和辅助质量指标，并具有竞争性的质量-效率权衡。Loop Guidance在所有三个测试模型上进一步提升了两个主要指标；在Scale-RAE DiT2.4B上，GenEval从0.4471提升至0.5691，DPG-Bench从0.7656提升至0.8053。

Conclusion: 该训练免费的循环框架为flow-matching图像生成器提供了一种新的测试时计算利用方式，通过向冻结的去噪器内部注入额外计算来提升生成质量，为高质量图像生成提供了一条不依赖采样步数增加的替代路径。

Abstract: We aim to improve frozen flow-matching image generators by adding inference computation inside the denoiser, without changing model weights or the outer sampler. Existing generators usually spend extra test-time computation by increasing the number of sampling steps, which repeatedly evaluates the entire denoiser and couples quality gains to sampler cost. A key challenge is how to use extra computation inside a frozen transformer denoiser: the method must decide which tokens, layers, and sampling times receive repeated updates while preserving the original generation pipeline. We introduce a training-free looping framework that repeatedly applies selected transformer layers inside each denoising call. Dense and Sparse Token Loop vary the token scope; Sampling-Progress Gating and the loop layer range specify when and where looping is active; loop count and strength control the repeated updates; and Loop Guidance combines ordinary and looped vector-field predictions. Across two Scale-RAE model scales, loop variants improve primary and auxiliary quality metrics with competitive quality--efficiency trade-offs. Loop Guidance further improves both primary metrics across all three tested models; on Scale-RAE DiT2.4B, it raises GenEval from 0.4471 to 0.5691 and DPG-Bench from 0.7656 to 0.8053. Code will be released.

</details>


### [65] [Subtraction-Based Tumor Segmentation and Lesion-Centered pCR Prediction for the MAMA-MIA Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29162)
*Kai Geissler,Raphael Schäfer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FME团队参加了MAMA-MIA挑战赛，使用残差编码器nnU-Net集成模型进行乳腺肿瘤分割，并集成25个预训练3D视频分类器进行病理完全缓解（pCR）预测，在两个任务中均排名第二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估在外部多国队列的预处理动态对比增强乳腺MRI上，肿瘤分割和pCR预测方法的性能与公平性，尤其是在跨中心域偏移条件下的鲁棒性。

Method: 分割任务使用五折残差编码器nnU-Net集成，输入仅采用第一张造影后减去造影前的图像，结合镜像测试时增强和最大连通域滤波；pCR预测集成25个预训练3D视频分类器，输入为病变中心裁剪的造影前和两个造影后体积。

Result: 分割任务综合性能公平性得分0.882，Dice系数0.713，归一化Hausdorff距离0.099；pCR预测综合得分0.664，平衡准确率0.541，等优势差距0.212。两个任务均排名第二。

Conclusion: 基于减影的输入和集成方法能够支持跨站点域偏移下的稳健肿瘤分割，而单纯基于基线DCE-MRI的pCR预测能力仍然有限。

Abstract: We describe the submission of team FME to the MAMA-MIA Challenge, which evaluated primary tumor segmentation and prediction of pathological complete response (pCR) from pretreatment dynamic contrast-enhanced breast MRI on an external multi-country cohort. For segmentation, we trained a five-fold residual-encoder nnU-Net ensemble using only the first post-contrast minus pre-contrast image, combined with mirroring test-time augmentation and largest-connected-component filtering. For pCR prediction, we ensembled 25 pretrained 3D video classifiers trained on lesion-centred crops from the pre-contrast and first two post-contrast volumes. FME ranked second in both tasks. The segmentation method achieved a combined performance-fairness score of 0.882, with Dice 0.713 and normalized Hausdorff distance 0.099. The pCR method achieved a combined score of 0.664, balanced accuracy of 0.541, and equalized-odds disparity of 0.212. The results indicate that subtraction-based input and ensembling support robust tumor segmentation under cross-site domain shift, whereas pCR prediction from baseline DCE-MRI alone remains limited.
  For the submission repository, see https://github.com/FraunhoferMEVIS/MAMA-MIA-Challenge-FME

</details>


### [66] [Mapping-Based Image Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29164)
*Freddie Åström,Michael Felsberg,George Baravdish*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于张量的新型泛函用于目标图像增强和去噪，通过显式正则化将应用相关信息和上下文知识融入变分模型，并证明了极小值存在性及相关几何性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中很少有变分模型能够同时描述应用相关信息（如gamma校正、目标值域滤波）和去噪问题的上下文知识，缺乏将两者结合的统一框架。

Method: 提出一种基于张量的变分泛函，通过显式正则化将应用相关与上下文信息纳入模型中，并分析了张量对称性约束、凸性及几何解释。

Result: 在含非线性函数的应用（如gamma校正和目标值域滤波）中表现出色，在通用去噪性能上与基于PDE的最先进方法相当。

Conclusion: 该张量框架为图像增强与去噪提供了统一的理论基础，兼具应用针对性和通用去噪能力。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce a novel tensor-based functional for targeted image enhancement and denoising. Via explicit regularization, our formulation incorporates application dependent and contextual information using first principles. Few works in literature treat variational models that describe both application dependent information and contextual knowledge of the denoising problem. We prove the existence of a minimizer and present results on tensor symmetry constraints, convexity, and geometric interpretation of the proposed functional. We show that our framework excels in applications where nonlinear functions are present such as in gamma correction and targeted value range filtering. We also study general denoising performance where we show comparable results to dedicated PDE-based state of the art methods.

</details>


### [67] [Feature-Spectral Fragility in Segmentation: Dataset Dependence, Architecture-Specific Localization, and Spectral Correlates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29167)
*Subhash Kashyap*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究通过后训练低通滤波干预三种分割架构（CNN、SSM、Transformer）的内部特征表示，发现特征域频谱鲁棒性具有强烈的数据集依赖性和架构特异性，且与输入域频谱鲁棒性截然不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分割模型鲁棒性评估多聚焦于输入域扰动，而对学习到的特征表示中频率内容的依赖性理解不足，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在CVC-ClinicDB和ISIC2018两个数据集上，对ResNet50-UNet（CNN）、VM-UNet（SSM）和Swin-UNETR（Transformer）三种架构实施后训练特征域低通滤波干预，进行单阶段干预定位敏感深度，并测量原生特征域频谱，同时评估傅里叶增强的效果。

Result: 在rho=0.25时，CVC上CNN、SSM和Transformer的Dice分别下降100%、73.2%和30.9%，而ISIC上仅下降9.4%、10.3%和0.6%，跨数据集差异在统计上显著。敏感性定位于架构特定深度：CNN在中/后期编码器块，SSM在早期编码器阶段。高频能量与脆弱性在CVC上呈负相关，在ISIC上仅部分相关。傅里叶增强改善输入域鲁棒性但对特征域退化几乎无效。

Conclusion: 特征频谱鲁棒性强烈依赖数据集、具有架构特异性，并且与输入域频谱鲁棒性相互独立，高低频能量与脆弱性的关系仅作为候选相关因素而非已验证的机制。

Abstract: Robustness of segmentation models is commonly assessed through input-domain perturbations, while dependence on frequency content within learned feature representations remains less understood. We probe this dependence using targeted post-training low-pass interventions on internal representations of three segmentation architectures, ResNet50-UNet (CNN), VM-UNet (SSM), and Swin-UNETR (Transformer), across CVC-ClinicDB and ISIC2018, with headline evaluations performed on untouched held-out test sets. At cutoff rho=0.25, feature-domain low-pass filtering causes severe degradation on CVC: Dice drops by 100%, 73.2%, and 30.9% for CNN, SSM, and Transformer, respectively, compared with 9.4%, 10.3%, and 0.6% on ISIC. The cross-dataset difference is statistically significant for every architecture. Single-stage interventions further show that sensitivity is localized at architecture-specific depths: the CNN peaks at a mid/late encoder block, whereas the SSM peaks in an early encoder stage on both datasets. Native feature-domain spectral measurements show an inverse association between high-frequency energy and fragility on CVC; the relationship is only partial on ISIC and is therefore treated as a candidate correlate rather than a proven mechanism. Finally, Fourier augmentation improves robustness to input-space low-pass filtering but leaves feature-domain degradation essentially unchanged. These results show that feature-spectral robustness is strongly dataset-dependent, architecture-specific, and distinct from input-domain spectral robustness.

</details>


### [68] [A Tensor Variational Formulation of Gradient Energy Total Variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29172)
*Freddie Åström,George Baravdish,Michael Felsberg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于张量的全变分新变分方法——梯度能量全变分（GETV），引入梯度能量张量并证明其对应的Euler-Lagrange方程是张量型全变分偏微分方程，同时证明GETV是凸泛函。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于结构张量的方法无法形式化推导出对应的Euler-Lagrange方程，这一局限性阻碍了张量型变分去噪方法的理论分析和实际应用，因此需要一种能支持形式化推导的新方案。

Method: 将梯度能量张量引入到全变分框架中构建GETV模型；通过数学推导获得对应的Euler-Lagrange方程；并给出GETV为凸泛函的严格证明。

Result: 实验结果表明，GETV在灰度图像和彩色图像去噪任务中，性能优于增强各向异性扩散（EAD）和传统全变分（TV）等现有先进变分去噪方法。

Conclusion: GETV作为一种新的凸张量型变分方法，不仅克服了传统结构张量无法形式化推导E-L方程的局限，还在去噪效果上超越了现有主流方法，具有良好的理论研究价值和实际应用前景。

Abstract: We present a novel variational approach to a tensor-based total variation formulation which is called gradient energy total variation, GETV. We introduce the gradient energy tensor [6] into the GETV and show that the corresponding Euler-Lagrange (E-L) equation is a tensor-based partial differential equation of total variation type. Furthermore, we give a proof which shows that GETV is a convex functional. This approach, in contrast to the commonly used structure tensor, enables a formal derivation of the corresponding E-L equation. Experimental results suggest that GETV compares favourably to other state of the art variational denoising methods such as extended anisotropic diffusion (EAD)[1] and total variation (TV) [18] for gray-scale and colour images.

</details>


### [69] [Dynamic-Robust Photometric-Semantic Reconstruction for Open-Vocabulary 3D Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29177)
*Boyu Cai,Li Yang,Yan Xu,Wei Liu,Nian Liu,Sikui Zhang,Yan Wang,Chunfeng Yuan,Weiming Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SPAR，一种联合语义-几何编码架构，通过在潜在空间聚合前显式隔离瞬态动态噪声，并结合动态区域感知的端到端训练范式，解决开放词汇分割与新视图合成在动态环境中特征错位的问题。在D-RE10K基准上达到SOTA性能，仅用3/4个输入视图即可获得22.15/23.33 dB的PSNR，自监督训练下运动掩码预测mIoU达88.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将新视图合成（NVS）与开放词汇分割（OVS）结合的3D基础模型受限于静态场景假设，在无约束动态环境中会导致空间特征严重错位，需要弥补这一关键差距。

Method: 提出SPAR联合语义-几何编码架构，在潜在空间聚合前显式隔离瞬态动态噪声；同时引入动态区域感知的端到端训练范式，将运动估计与多视角视觉及语义学习结构性地耦合，使网络从动态输入中蒸馏多视角一致、时间稳定的场景表示。

Result: 在D-RE10K基准上达到SOTA；3视图和4视图输入下PSNR分别为22.15 dB和23.33 dB；自监督训练下运动掩码预测mIoU达88.5%。分析还揭示光度重建与语义理解之间存在强任务协同效应，语义合成学习能持续提升新视图渲染的光度保真度。

Conclusion: SPAR通过显式动态噪声隔离与端到端动态感知训练，成功统一了动态场景下的新视图合成与开放词汇分割，且任务间存在正向协同，代码已开源。

Abstract: The integration of novel view synthesis (NVS) and open-vocabulary segmentation (OVS) has recently yielded powerful feed-forward 3D foundation models. However, their inherent reliance on static-scene assumptions leads to severe misalignment of spatial features in unconstrained dynamic environments. To bridge this critical gap, we propose SPAR, a novel joint semantic-geometric encoding architecture that explicitly isolates transient dynamic noise prior to latent space aggregation. Furthermore, we introduce a dynamic-region-aware end-to-end training paradigm that structurally couples motion estimation with multi-view visual and semantic learning. This unified approach enables the network to inherently resolve motion conflicts and distill multi-view consistent, temporally stable scene representations from dynamic inputs. Extensive experiments on the challenging D-RE10K benchmark demonstrate that SPAR achieves state-of-the-art performance. Our end-to-end approach achieves exceptional novel view synthesis quality, yielding a PSNR of 22.15 dB and 23.33 dB given only 3 and 4 input views respectively. Despite being trained in a self-supervised manner, our model achieves an mIoU of 88.5% for motion mask prediction. Furthermore, our analysis reveals a strong inter-task synergy between photometric scene reconstruction and semantic understanding, where semantic synthesis learning consistently enhances photometric fidelity in novel view rendering. Code will be available at https://github.com/dmucby/SPAR.

</details>


### [70] [Foundational feature fusion for conditional flow matching in 6D pose estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29183)
*Amir Hamza,Davide Boscaini,Fabio Poiesi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FunFlow6D提出了一种基于条件流匹配的物体6D姿态估计方法，利用几何和外观基础模型的特征替代任务特定的编码器，并引入基于交叉注意力的特征融合机制，在BOP基准的四个数据集上超越了现有最先进方法，同时降低了监督需求和内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于条件流匹配的6D姿态估计方法需要训练任务特定的编码器，并依赖于简单的特征融合策略来解决姿态歧义，这增加了训练成本和复杂性。

Method: FunFlow6D采用流匹配框架，直接利用几何与外观基础模型提取的特征作为条件，无需训练任务特定编码器；同时提出基于交叉注意力的融合机制，动态结合几何和外观特征，为流匹配模块提供更丰富的条件信息。

Result: 在BOP基准的四个数据集上，FunFlow6D超越了此前的最先进方法，同时减少了监督需求与内存开销；消融实验验证了各组成部分的有效性。

Conclusion: FunFlow6D证明了利用预训练基础模型特征配合交叉注意力融合，可以在降低训练成本的同时提升6D姿态估计性能，为该任务提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Conditional flow matching has enabled a step forward in object 6D pose estimation, achieving state-of-the-art performance by progressively denoising and registering object representations to observed scenes. Existing methods require training task-specific encoders supervised on object-scene overlap and rely on trivial feature fusion strategies to resolve pose ambiguities. We present FunFlow6D, a novel flow matching-based formulation that leverages features from geometric and appearance foundation models for pose estimation, eliminating the need for task-specific encoder training. We also introduce a cross attention-based fusion mechanism that dynamically combines geometric and appearance features to provide richer conditioning for the flow matching module. Experiments on four datasets from the BOP benchmark show that FunFlow6D outperforms the previous state of the art while reducing supervision requirements and memory overhead. Extensive ablations validate the contribution of each proposed component. Project website: https://tev-fbk.github.io/FunFlow6D/.

</details>


### [71] [Multi-Scale Temporal Domain Alignment for Federated Video Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29186)
*Lee En-Yi Hannah,Haozhi Cao,Yuecong Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出METAL框架，通过多尺度时间域对齐解决联邦视频域适应（FVDA）中的时间信息对齐难题，在多个数据集上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦视频域适应（FVDA）在分布式非独立同分布视频数据集上进行协作学习的同时保护隐私，但该领域研究不足，主要挑战在于跨域时间信息的对齐。

Method: METAL框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 在源客户端训练多尺度Transformer编码器；(2) 在每个时间尺度上进行独立知识投票生成鲁棒伪标签，并用L2方差惩罚强制跨尺度一致性； (3) 通过知识蒸馏训练晚期融合头，利用置信度加权的尺度级预测聚合来融合互补的时间信息。

Result: 在Epic-Kitchens-55和Daily-DA数据集上达到最先进性能，相比现有FDA方法最高提升28.47%。消融实验证明多尺度蒸馏和尺度协调对时间知识迁移至关重要。

Conclusion: 多尺度时间信息的利用和跨尺度一致性协调是提升联邦视频域适应性能的关键，METAL通过仅模型参数传递的方式有效实现了跨域视频动作识别。

Abstract: Federated Video Domain Adaptation (FVDA) enables collaborative learning across distributed and non-IID video datasets while preserving privacy, but is under-explored due to challenges in aligning temporal information. We propose Multi-scalE Temporal domAin aLignment (METAL), a novel framework that leverages temporal information at multiple resolutions to improve cross-domain video action recognition with only model parameter transfers. METAL trains per-scale transformer encoders on source-clients, then performs independent knowledge voting at each temporal scale to generate robust pseudo-labels on the target-server. A novel $L_2$ variance penalty enforces cross-scale consistency during scale-based knowledge distillation, preventing a singular dominant scale. The late fusion aggregates features across different scales, where the fusion head is trained via knowledge distillation using confidence-weighted aggregation of scale-wise predictions, enabling the model to effectively exploit complementary temporal information for final predictions. Experiments on Epic-Kitchens-55 and Daily-DA demonstrate state-of-the-art performances, with gains up to 28.47% over current FDA methods. Ablation studies prove that multi-scale distillation and scale coordination are critical for effective temporal knowledge transfer.

</details>


### [72] [OPUS-V2: Bridging the Gap between Sparse Points and Dense Voxels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29187)
*Jiabao Wang,Qiang Meng,Liujiang Yan,Ke Wang,Qibin Hou,Ming-Ming Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OPUS-V2是一个基于点云稀疏表示的3D占据预测框架，通过引入轻量级点-体素变换（PVT）模块，将稀疏预测自适应映射到密集体素空间，消除了训练和推理中的人工启发式操作，并在特征生成与占据生成解耦后支持任意占据分辨率。在Occ3D上达到44.0 rayIoU，在OpenOccupancy上达到16.4 mIoU并支持20.6 FPS实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于点的占据预测范式虽然实现了精度与效率的良好平衡，但其稀疏预测结果与自动驾驶系统所需的密集体素占据表示之间存在固有失配，需要依赖训练和推理阶段的手工启发式方法来弥补，这限制了最终性能的上限。

Method: OPUS-V2在OPUS框架基础上，在解码器后引入轻量级点-体素变换（PVT）模块，自适应地将稀疏预测映射到密集体素空间；同时将特征生成与占据生成过程解耦，使模型能够适应任意占据分辨率。

Result: 在Occ3D数据集上达到44.0的rayIoU（当前最优），在更具挑战性的OpenOccupancy数据集上达到16.4 mIoU，并实现20.6 FPS的实时推理速度。

Conclusion: OPUS-V2通过消除手工启发式操作并解耦特征与占据生成，有效解决了稀疏到密集映射的失配问题，在保持实时性能的同时显著提升了占据预测精度。

Abstract: The point-based occupancy prediction paradigm has achieved an attractive trade-off between accuracy and efficiency by modeling 3D space sparsely. However, its predictions inherently mismatch the dense voxel-based occupancy required by self-driving systems, necessitating hand-crafted heuristics during training and inference that limit final performance. To overcome these limitations, we propose OPUS-V2, a novel framework built upon the pioneering OPUS (occupancy prediction using a sparse set) point-based approach. OPUS-V2 incorporates a lightweight point-voxel transformation (PVT) module behind the decoder to adaptively map sparse predictions into the dense voxel space, eliminating the need for suboptimal operations and improving model accuracy. Furthermore, our architecture decouples feature and occupancy generation processes, allowing OPUS-V2 to adapt to arbitrary occupancy resolutions. OPUS-V2 achieves a state-of-the-art rayIoU of 44.0 on the Occ3D dataset. On the more challenging OpenOccupancy dataset, it attains a competitive 16.4 mIoU while running in real time at 20.6 FPS.

</details>


### [73] [RLG-TPV: Radar- and LiDAR-Guided Tri-Perspective View Fusion for Camera-Radar 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29194)
*Ahmet Mete Dokgoz,A. Enes Doruk,Hasan F. Ates*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RLG-TPV，一种基于三透视视图（TPV）表示的多模态相机-雷达3D目标检测框架。通过射线引导的可变形注意力机制，利用LiDAR监督的深度概率和雷达视锥占用为图像特征提供几何引导；此外引入多普勒引导的时间融合和RCS感知的雷达散射模块。在nuScenes上达到0.4981 mAP和0.5959 NDS，相比CRN基线将朝向误差和速度误差分别降低31.9%和30.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TPV提升方法主要基于相机，导致沿投影相机射线的采样图像证据深度存在模糊性。作者希望引入雷达和训练时的LiDAR提供互补的几何引导，以增强3D场景表征的深度准确性。

Method: 1) 提出RLG-TPV多模态TPV框架，结合相机、雷达和训练时LiDAR；2) 射线引导的可变形注意力提升机制，利用LiDAR监督的相机深度概率和雷达视锥占用加权采样图像特征；3) LiDAR导出的类别占用目标监督侧平面和前平面训练，推理时移除对应头，仅需相机和雷达；4) 多普勒引导的时间融合，利用雷达径向速度锚定运动场并配合门控限制无效区域扭曲；5) RCS感知的雷达散射模块，使雷达证据按雷达散射截面条件在空间邻域传播。

Result: 在nuScenes验证集上达到0.4981 mAP和0.5959 NDS，相比CRN基线将朝向误差和速度误差分别降低31.9%和30.7%。消融实验表明射线级几何引导是最终性能的主要贡献因素。

Conclusion: RLG-TPV验证了多模态几何引导（雷达+训练时LiDAR）对TPV表示构建的有效性，在推理时无需LiDAR即可获得显著性能提升，同时证明了射线级几何引导和雷达物理属性（多普勒、RCS）利用的重要性。

Abstract: Tri-Perspective View (TPV) representations describe 3D scene structure through top, side, and front feature planes, but existing TPV lifting is primarily camera-based, leaving the depth of sampled image evidence ambiguous along projected camera rays. We propose RLG-TPV, a multimodal TPV framework for camera-radar 3D object detection in which radar and training-time LiDAR provide complementary geometric guidance during representation construction. A ray-guided deformable-attention lift weights sampled image features using LiDAR-supervised camera depth probabilities and radar frustum occupancy, while radar additionally refines the depth distribution before lifting. Because conventional radar provides limited elevation information, LiDAR-derived class-occupancy targets supervise the side and front planes during training; the corresponding heads are removed at inference, so deployment requires only cameras and radar. For temporal aggregation, Doppler-guided temporal fusion aligns past features using a motion field anchored by measured radar radial velocity, with gating that limits warping in regions without supported motion. An RCS-aware radar scatter further allows radar evidence to spread over spatial neighborhoods conditioned on radar cross section. On the nuScenes validation set, RLG-TPV achieves 0.4981 mAP and 0.5959 NDS, reducing orientation and velocity error by 31.9\% and 30.7\% relative to the published CRN baseline. Ablation studies show that ray-level geometric guidance is a major contributor to the final performance.

</details>


### [74] [Bayesian-Optimized Superpixel-GrabCut for Traceable Optic Disc Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29196)
*Shraddha Changune,Vivek Noel Soren,Gautam Das,Tapan Kumar Gandhi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种完全算法可追踪且可训练的视盘分割流水线，结合超像素分解、混合亮度-邻近性评分、形态学正则化、迭代GrabCut细化和椭圆拟合，在Drishti-GS数据集上达到0.9536的Dice系数，与最先进方法性能相当，同时提供全过程的数学透明性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习视盘分割方法作为黑盒模型，缺乏推理阶段的可数学追踪性，无法满足临床工作流中对算法审计和故障分析的需求。

Method: 将超像素分解、混合亮度-邻近性超像素评分、形态学正则化、迭代GrabCut细化和椭圆形状拟合组合成一个统一的、可训练的流水线；超参数优化被构建为目标函数并通过贝叶斯优化求解，以消除手动调参。

Result: 在Drishti-GS数据集上的定量评估显示，该方法实现了0.9536的Dice系数，与最先进的性能相匹配。

Conclusion: 通过在所有处理阶段保持明确的数学透明性，该框架为医学审查和调试提供了一种确定性的、可追踪的黑盒架构替代方案。

Abstract: Optic disc (OD) segmentation is essential for diagnosing ophthalmic pathologies from retinal fundus images. However, prevailing deep learning approaches operate as opaque black boxes, lacking the inference-stage mathematical traceability--a critical requirement for algorithmic auditing and failure analysis in clinical workflows. This paper presents a fully algorithmically traceable and trainable segmentation pipeline that jointly combines superpixel decomposition, hybrid brightness-proximity superpixel scoring, morphological regularization, iterative GrabCut refinement, and elliptical shape fitting. The hyperparameter optimization is formulated as an objective function and solved via Bayesian optimization to eliminate manual parameter tuning. A quantitative evaluation on the Drishti-GS dataset demonstrates that our method achieves a Dice coefficient of 0.9536, matching state-of-the-art performance. By maintaining explicit mathematical transparency across all processing stages, our framework offers a deterministic, traceable alternative to black-box architectures for medical review and debugging.

</details>


### [75] [Ground-to-Satellite Localization in Unconstrained Image Collections for 3D Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29211)
*Angel Daruna,Ben Southall,Niluthpol Chowdhury Mithun,Kshitij Minhas,Nicholas Meegan,Qiao Wang,Bogdan Matei,Supun Samarasekera,Rakesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种鲁棒的层级式跨视角定位框架，利用地面图像集合的SfM模型与卫星图像的几何约束，实现无需全景图或已知初始位置的精确定位，并通过KDE聚合预测以过滤异常值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨视角定位方法要求全景图像或已知初始位置，限制了其在野外无约束图像集合重建场景中的应用。

Method: 通过SfM模型提取几何约束，采用粗到细的跨视角匹配生成位姿假设，并利用核密度估计（KDE）聚合多个预测以恢复共识对齐并过滤离群值。

Result: 实验表明该方法在具有挑战性的图像集合上能可靠定位，并能实现精确的尺度估计、重复检测和不相交SfM重建的合并。

Conclusion: 卫星参考对齐能显著提升SfM重建的完整性和地理定位精度，获得比单独使用SfM更完整的场地模型。

Abstract: Ground image localization with respect to satellite imagery is a key enabler for metrically-accurate, geo-localized 3D scene reconstruction from unconstrained image collections. Existing cross-view localization methods have strict requirements such as panoramic imagery or known initial locations, limiting their applicability for in-the-wild reconstruction settings. We propose a robust hierarchical cross-view localization framework that leverages geometric constraints from Structure-from-Motion (SfM) models derived from unconstrained ground image collections. Our method generates coarse-to-fine pose hypotheses through a cross-view matching approach and aggregates noisy predictions across SfM model(s) using Kernel Density Estimation to recover consensus alignments while filtering outliers. Experiments demonstrate reliable localization performance from challenging image collections. Empirically we found satellite-referenced alignment enables accurate metric scale estimation, doppelgänger detection, and merging of disjoint SfM reconstructions, resulting in more complete, geo-localized site models than are possible with SfM alone.

</details>


### [76] [SGPDFuse: Semantically-Guided Physics-Disentanglement General Multi-Modal Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29220)
*Haozhen Wei,Chengjun Jiang,Yutong Guo,Xinrui Ju,Xingyuan Li,Xiang Chen,Jinyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SGPDFuse，通过语义-物理参数桥（SPPB）将多模态图像映射到物理解耦的结构表示中，利用固有-变化原理分离场景本质属性与瞬态环境因素，结合语义对齐机制在基础模型特征空间中保持关键目标并通过Gram矩阵正则化去除伪影，在多个融合基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MMIF方法依赖盲特征聚合，擅长信号积累但无法区分本质内容与物理退化，缺乏对场景固有属性与环境干扰的显式解耦能力。

Method: 1) 基于预训练视觉基础模型构建语义-物理参数桥（SPPB），将输入映射为物理解耦的结构表示；2) 利用固有-变化（Intrinsic-Variation）原理解耦不变场景属性与瞬态环境因素；3) 引入语义对齐机制：通过余弦相似度锚定融合表示与基础模型特征空间中的显著语义特征，保障关键目标；4) 使用Gram矩阵正则化强化物理纹理保真度，消除不自然伪影。

Result: 在红外-可见光、多聚焦、多曝光三个基准上，使用单一架构均达到最先进（SOTA）性能。

Conclusion: 通过物理感知的语义解耦与对齐机制，SGPDFuse有效弥合了特征聚合与物理退化区分之间的鸿沟，验证了单一架构跨多模态融合任务的泛化能力。

Abstract: Multimodal image fusion (MMIF) aims to integrate complementary sensor data into a single representation that preserves intrinsic scene reality while eliminating environmental interferences. Most existing approaches rely on blind feature aggregation, which excels at signal accumulation but fails to distinguish essential content from physical degradations. We propose SGPDFuse, which bridges this gap by mapping inputs into a physics-disentangled structural representation via a Semantic-Physical Parametric Bridge (SPPB) built on pretrained vision foundation models, utilizing the Intrinsic-Variation principle to decouple invariant scene attributes from transient environmental factors. To guide this decomposition, we introduce a Semantic Alignment mechanism: we explicitly anchor the fused representation to salient semantic features in the same foundation model feature space via cosine similarity to preserve critical targets, while enforcing physical texture fidelity through Gram-matrix regularization to strictly eliminate unnatural artifacts. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SGPDFuse achieves state-of-the-art performance across infrared-visible, multi-focus, and multi-exposure benchmarks using a single architecture.

</details>


### [77] [Using Channel Representations in Regularization Terms: A Case Study on Image Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29227)
*Christian Heinemann,Freddie Åström,George Baravdish,Kai Krajsek,Michael Felsberg,Hanno Scharr*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于通道表示的非线性扩散滤波方法，通过软直方图表示图像像素邻域，构建新的能量泛函，导出带加权项的非线性鲁棒扩散方案，在混合高斯和脉冲噪声（如缺失数据）的图像重建中表现良好，去噪性能与主流方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散滤波方法在处理混合噪声（高斯噪声与脉冲噪声组合）时性能有限，需要一种新的框架来更有效地处理此类复杂噪声场景。

Method: 基于通道编码获得像素邻域的软直方图表示，据此构建新的能量泛函；求解其Euler-Lagrange方程，导出带通道表示加权项的非线性鲁棒扩散更新方案。

Result: 在图像重建任务中，该方法在混合高斯与脉冲噪声（含缺失数据）下表现良好；在常见标量图像去噪实验中，性能与其他扩散方案及当前最优去噪方法相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 基于通道表示的扩散滤波方法为混合噪声下的图像重建提供了有效且具有竞争性的解决方案。

Abstract: In this work we propose a novel non-linear diffusion filtering approach for images based on their channel representation. To derive the diffusion update scheme we formulate a novel energy functional using a soft-histogram representation of image pixel neighborhoods obtained from the channel encoding. The resulting Euler-Lagrange equation yields a non-linear robust diffusion scheme with additional weighting terms stemming from the channel representation which steer the diffusion process. We apply this novel energy formulation to image reconstruction problems, showing good performance in the presence of mixtures of Gaussian and impulse-like noise, e.g. missing data. In denoising experiments of common scalar-valued images our approach performs competitive compared to other diffusion schemes as well as state-of-the-art denoising methods for the considered noise types.

</details>


### [78] [Compact Snapshot Spectral Imaging with Calibration-Free Aperture Diffraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29230)
*Tao Lv,Quan Yuan,Shiqiao Li,Chenglong Huang,Linsen Chen,Chongde Zi,Shuming Wang,Xun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种紧凑、低成本、免标定的快照光谱成像方法ADIS，仅由衍射透镜、二元掩模和Bayer滤波传感器组成，无需额外物理空间即可实现全分辨率光谱重建，并配套提出ODAUVST网络以高效求解逆问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快照光谱成像（SSI）能够提供高维时空-光谱观测以揭示物体内在物理特性，但其复杂系统和重复标定需求限制了边缘应用场景的部署。

Method: 提出Aperture Diffraction Imaging Spectrometer (ADIS)，仅由衍射透镜+二元掩模+Bayer传感器构成；利用理论计算PSF实现免标定重建；引入Orthogonal Diffraction-Aware Unfolding Framework (ODAUF)框架和Voxel Shift Transformer (VST)模块，组合成ODAUVST网络用于稀疏约束逆问题的求解。

Result: 通过理论推导、系统性对比实验和真实快照光谱成像实验，验证了ADIS方法的优越性，实现了在商用相机尺寸内的免标定全分辨率光谱成像，且模型参数量更少、重建效果优异。

Conclusion: ADIS成功缩小了仿真与现实的差距，兼顾了紧凑性、成本效益和标定自由，在商用相机物理尺寸内实现了全分辨率快照光谱成像，为边缘场景的光谱成像应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Snapshot Spectral Imaging (SSI) provides high-dimensional temporal-spatial-spectral observation to uncover intrinsic physical characteristics. However, its complex system and repetitive calibration requirements hinder edge applications. Here, we propose a compact, cost-effective, calibration-free SSI method, Aperture Diffraction Imaging Spectrometer (ADIS), which consists only of a diffractive lens with a binary mask and a Bayer-filtered sensor, requiring no additional physical footprint compared to standard RGB cameras. ADIS disperses and multiplexes wavelengths, mapping energy to distinct sensor locations, enabling full-resolution recovery from superpixel-level encodings. ADIS directly leverages theoretically computed PSFs to enable calibration-free spectral reconstruction, while tolerating lens-dependent variations across different optical configurations and bridging the gap between simulation and reality. To achieve SSI by solving a sparsely-constrained inverse problem, we introduce the Orthogonal Diffraction-Aware Unfolding Framework (ODAUF) with Voxel Shift Transformer (VST) for improved orthogonal diffraction perception. Integrating VST into ODAUF forms the efficient Orthogonal Diffraction-Aware Unfolding Voxel Shift Transformer (ODAUVST), delivering excellent recovery and reduced parameters. By elaborating on theory, systematic and comprehensive comparing, and demonstrating real SSI results, we validate the superiority of ADIS, achieving calibration-free full-resolution SSI within a commercial camera footprint.

</details>


### [79] [Background-Free Objectness Learning for Class-Agnostic Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29232)
*Dania Batool,Liliana Lo Presti,Marco La Cascia,Filippo Vella*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出背景无关目标性学习框架（B-FOR），一种密集类别无关检测方法，通过不依赖未标注区域的显式背景监督来学习目标性，在PASCAL VOC、MS-COCO和Open Images上较先前基线提升超过+10 AR点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在封闭集监督下训练的目标检测器将未标注区域隐式视为背景，当标注不完整时会产生目标性偏差——视觉上有效但未标注的物体被当作负样本，使目标性检测与标注类别体系绑定而非通用物体结构，这一问题在类别无关和开放世界检测中尤为突出。

Method: B-FOR将检测公式化为预测密集多尺度目标中心和尺度场，目标假设作为局部空间结构自然涌现；监督仅约束可靠标注区域，通过空间结构化的软目标避免前景-背景判别；引入位移感知尺度场，将物体范围建模为学习到的目标性场的空间变化属性，以支持从涌现局部极大值进行解码。

Result: 在PASCAL VOC、MS-COCO和Open Images上的实验表明，B-FOR对未见类别和跨数据集物体分布具有强泛化能力，相较先前的类别无关基线在召回率上提升超过+10 AR点。

Conclusion: 消融实验表明，局部化的目标性监督和位移感知尺度场对于在不完整标注下进行类别无关定位都至关重要，两者缺一不可。

Abstract: Object detectors are typically trained under closed-set supervision, where unlabeled regions are implicitly treated as background. Under incomplete annotations, this assumption introduces objectness bias: visually valid but unlabeled objects are used as negatives, tying objectness to the annotated taxonomy rather than generic object structure. This limitation is particularly problematic for class-agnostic and open-world detection. This paper proposes Background-Free Objectness Learning (B-FOR), a dense class-agnostic detection framework that learns objectness without explicit background supervision on unlabeled regions. B-FOR formulates detection as the prediction of dense multi-scale object-center and scale fields, from which object hypotheses emerge as local spatial structures. Supervision is confined to reliable annotated regions through spatially structured soft targets, avoiding foreground-background discrimination. To support decoding from emergent local maxima, the paper further introduces displacement-aware scale fields that model object extent as a spatially varying property of the learned objectness field. Experiments on PASCAL VOC, MS-COCO, and Open Images demonstrate strong generalization to unseen categories and cross-dataset object distributions. B-FOR improves recall by more than +10 AR points over prior class-agnostic baselines. Ablation studies show that both localized objectness supervision and displacement-aware scale fields are critical for class-agnostic localization under incomplete annotations. Code available at: https://github.com/Daniaawan/B-FOR.

</details>


### [80] [Generalization over Memorization: Generalization-Aware Diffusion Adaptation for Single-Image Multi-View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29233)
*Jie Li,Xingchen Zou,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ACM Multimedia 2026大挑战赛"单图引导多视角图像合成"的冠军解决方案GoM（Generalization over Memorization），在293支注册队伍中排名第一。该方案针对共享训练/验证场景导致的模型选择失败问题，提出了场景分离验证、曝光匹配选择和有针对性扩散适配等策略，并结合4B整流流DiT的LoRA微调等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战赛仅提供40个训练场景，需要从单张RGB模型生成26个目标视角，且禁止显式几何、外部渲染、链式生成、候选选择和后处理。作者识别出一个关键的模型选择失败问题：共享的训练和验证场景会使"记忆"被误认为"可迁移的视角控制能力"，因此在极小数据条件下需要重新思考验证设计。

Method: GoM框架包含三个核心组件：(1)场景分离验证（scene-disjoint validation），确保验证场景与训练不重叠；(2)曝光匹配选择（exposure-matched selection）；(3)有针对性的扩散适配。合成模型采用4B整流流DiT进行rank-32 LoRA适配，并结合优化器重启、晚期检查点平均和VAE解码器微调。

Result: 在293支注册队伍中排名第一，56支队伍在Phase-A公开排行榜上至少获得一次有效提交。进行了超过300次离线实验和24次在线提交，验证了验证设计和训练轨迹控制在小数据生成建模中的重要性可与架构规模相当。

Conclusion: 在小数据场景下，验证设计的正确性和训练轨迹控制对模型泛化能力的影响与架构规模同等重要，甚至更为关键。GoM框架证明了通过合理的验证策略和训练细节控制，可以在极小数据条件下实现强大的生成性能。

Abstract: We present the winning solution to the ACM Multimedia 2026 Grand Challenge on Single-Image Guided Multi-Angle Image Synthesis. It ranks first among 293 registered teams; 56 teams obtained at least one scored submission on the public Phase-A leaderboard. With only 40 training scenes, the challenge requires 26 target views from one RGB model and one forward pass per view; it prohibits explicit geometry, external rendering, chained generation, candidate selection, and post-processing. We identify a critical model-selection failure: shared training and validation scenes make memorization appear as transferable view control. We therefore introduce GoM. Short for Generalization over Memorization, the framework combines scene-disjoint validation, exposure-matched selection, and targeted diffusion adaptation. Its synthesis model adapts a 4B rectified-flow DiT using rank-32 LoRA, optimizer restarts, late-checkpoint averaging, and VAE decoder tuning. More than 300 offline experiments and 24 online submissions show that validation design and training-trajectory control can matter as much as architecture scale in small-data generative modeling.

</details>


### [81] [Uncertainty-Aware Multimodal Anti-UAV Detection via Evidential Fusion and Conflict-Discounted Belief Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29235)
*Sharanda Suttorp,Seyed Sahand Mohammadi Ziabari,Ali Mohammed Mansour Alsahag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文扩展EDTC到多模态RGB-热成像反无人机感知，提出基于折扣信念融合（DBF）的跨模态证据融合方法，在Anti-UAV基准上优于单流（测试准确率0.670 vs 0.604/0.598），但DBF与无折扣平均在经验上不可区分，因为基准中模态间冲突近乎为零；融合不确定性校准良好（ECE 0.057），但作为定位失败检测器弱于空间方差（AUROC 0.626 vs 0.739）。该结果为结构性零结果，基准的近普遍存在性和空编码特性共同抑制了模态间冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反无人机多模态系统以确定性方式融合RGB和热成像流，不建模预测不确定性，无法在流不一致时表达怀疑。虽然EDTC已利用证据深度学习实现热成像单模态的不确定性感知，但跨模态证据融合仍是空白，本文旨在填补这一缺口。

Method: 将EDTC扩展至多模态RGB-热成像感知，提出折扣信念融合（DBF）方法：在聚合各流意见前将模态间冲突转化为不确定性质量；边界框通过选择不确定性更低的模态来解析。在Anti-UAV基准上评估，并与无折扣平均基线对比。

Result: 多模态融合优于任一单流（测试准确率0.670 vs IR 0.604、RGB 0.598），实时速度至少38 FPS；但DBF与无折扣平均经验上不可区分，模态间冲突几乎为零使折扣机制失效；融合不确定性校准良好（ECE 0.057），但作为定位失败检测器弱于空间方差（AUROC 0.626 vs 0.739）。

Conclusion: 该零结果是结构性的：基准中目标近乎普遍存在且漏检编码方式空洞，共同抑制了模态间冲突的产生。这一诊断界定了冲突感知融合在何种场景下能提供可衡量的收益，为多模态证据融合方法的应用范围提供了重要边界条件。

Abstract: Anti-UAV perception systems must remain reliable when sensor streams degrade under occlusion, fast motion, or modality-specific failure. Existing multimodal anti-UAV systems fuse RGB and thermal streams deterministically, without modeling predictive uncertainty, and cannot express doubt when streams disagree. Evidential Deep Learning (EDL) produces calibrated per-class uncertainty in a single forward pass. EDTC already exploits this for thermal-only perception, yet cross-modal evidential fusion remains unaddressed.
  This paper extends EDTC to multimodal RGB-Thermal perception via Discounted Belief Fusion (DBF), which converts inter-modal conflict into uncertainty mass before aggregating stream opinions. Bounding boxes are resolved by selecting the lower-uncertainty modality. On the Anti-UAV benchmark, multimodal fusion consistently outperforms either single stream (test Acc 0.670 vs. 0.604 IR, 0.598 RGB) at real-time speed (at least 38 FPS). However, DBF is empirically indistinguishable from undiscounted averaging: near-zero inter-modal conflict on this presence-dominated benchmark leaves the discounting step inert. The fused uncertainty is well-calibrated (ECE 0.057) yet expectedly a weaker localization failure detector than spatial variance (AUROC 0.626 vs. 0.739). The null result is structural: the benchmark's near-universal presence and vacuous miss-encoding jointly suppress inter-modal conflict, a diagnosis that delimits where conflict-aware fusion provides measurable benefit.

</details>


### [82] [AGRICAM: A Track-Mounted Crop Pollination Monitoring Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29237)
*Malika Nisal Ratnayake,Adel N. Toosi,James Cook,Romina Rader,Alan Dorin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为AGRICAM的自主机器人系统，专为保护性栽培系统中的大规模授粉监测而设计。该系统沿低成本轨道自主运行，集成双RGB相机、微气候传感器、GPS、RFID等模块，通过4G网络将数据传至云端，利用计算机视觉模型量化授粉昆虫的访问活动。在商用蓝莓农场80米长的工业隧道中进行了30小时的部署测试，成功绘制了授粉模式图，验证了隧道内授粉者分布的均匀性，并揭示了时间与微气候对昆虫活动的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 昆虫授粉对全球粮食生产至关重要，但在商业农场规模上监测授粉者仍具挑战性。现有计算机视觉技术虽能进行详细分析，但监测需在细节、空间覆盖及人力资源之间权衡，缺乏可扩展的自动化解决方案。

Method: 构建AGRICAM自主机器人平台：沿低成本易安装轨道移动，集成RGB相机、微气候传感器、GPS、RFID、运动传感器和4G网络连接；通过网页界面远程配置；数据上传云端后用计算机视觉模型分析授粉者访问量和时空活动变化。在商用蓝莓农场的工业隧道中部署验证。
